# Rosa Alvarez — Voice, free arm

## 1. Assignment and limits

Rosa Alvarez, 62, retired school administrator from Puebla; busy second-language reader. First visit to Voice; source unread. Round HF3 Voice R1, arm free, paired session hf3-voice-r1-rosa-alvarez. Frozen diary: “No sessions recorded. This file contains no play evidence.” This is an alternative exposure, not a continuation of another arm.

URL: https://human.adamwhite.work/voice/. Visible title: The Day You Say Nothing. No build shown. Assigned device: 360×690 touch/light. Access: supplied HTTP player driver at localhost:19423, sequential curl POST operations. Floor 0, ceiling 25 real minutes. First screen at driver elapsed 0.479 s; play stopped at 10.322 s; required local feedback inspection ended at 20.712 s. No screenshot viewed, so physical layout is untested. No consent change, export, live note, feedback text, or submission. No player id shown.

## 2. First minute

I quit before 60 s. These are final observations at 10.322 s, not a 60-second checkpoint. No live checkpoint note was created because the owner prohibited live notes. The reactions below are recalled after stopping; they are not contemporaneous log entries.

| Item                       | Result and original checkpoint answer                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DL-1 A reason to act       | PASS — “I am helping Halil with his goals.” The introduction says “Help him reach his goals during Ramadan, the fasting month.” I had not opened the goals.                                                                            |
| DL-2 What can I touch      | PASS for text controls — New game, Information, Game menu, then Halil, His goals, Today, Speak and Skip were listed as controls. I tapped New game. Visual affordance NOT TESTED — not established by this harness.                    |
| DL-3 Urgency and selection | FAIL for my understanding — “I see a clock and fasting until 18:46, but I do not know if I must hurry.” Nothing I selected in play remained visibly selected.                                                                          |
| DL-4 Why the limit         | FAIL — “Starting…” was disabled initially; after a three-second wait it became New game. I did not know what it was waiting for. The later Continue tap met a changed page, not a disabled control.                                    |
| DL-5 Readable situation    | PASS — “Halil misses Nuran.” Visible cue: “I miss you, Nuran.” Speak was an available response.                                                                                                                                        |
| DL-6 A choice soon         | NOT TESTED for a meaningful suggestion choice. Speak was visible at 10.322 s, within 30 s. I never opened it. First input was New game at 8.571 s; the subsequent Continue tap at 10.322 s reported that the page had already changed. |
| DL-7 Learn by doing        | NOT TESTED — zero suggestion choices reached; zero suggestion consequences tested. New game reached play, but I did not learn what a suggestion meant by doing one.                                                                    |

## 3. Verbatim recalled diary and observed inputs

No live diary was written. The quoted first-person reactions here are my post-stop recollection in my own words, preserved without rewriting them after learning a rule. Times identify the corresponding driver observations, not measured reading durations.

- 0.479 s, open: Introduction and disabled Starting… shown. “I can help him, but I need to read the Eid sentence again.” Sentence reread 1: “On Eid, the festival at its end, he will act without your advice.” “I had to connect ‘its end’ with Ramadan and understand that I stop advising him then.”
- 6.404 s, wait 3: New game available. “Now I can begin.”
- 8.571 s, tap New game: Continue · Ramadan 1 · 03:40 appeared alongside New game. “I have just started. I will continue.”
- 10.322 s, tap Continue · Ramadan 1 · 03:40: reply showed the game at Ramadan 1 · Mon 11 Mar · 04:11, fasting until 18:46; scene timestamp 03:59. Driver answer: “Continue · Ramadan 1 · 03:40 is not on the page now; it was listed 2 s ago and the page has changed (game state, not a driver failure)”. Visible scene included “Halil remembers Nuran as he sits at the table.” and “I miss you, Nuran.” “I understand he misses her.”
- Same screen, reread 2: “You can wait without choosing a suggestion.” “Where are the suggestions? Does Speak give me suggestions, or do I have to say something myself?”
- 10.322 s, want_quit and play stop, CONFUSED: “That is my second reread. I would stop here.” This was a recalled quit at the second sentence, not a driver log operation. No further game action was taken.
- 16.535 s, required local inspection, tap Game menu: clock 04:59; held: menu open. Visible “Halil chose to pray at home, until 05:14.” and “Selin usually calls this evening.” Menu said “The day waits while this is open.” “The menu stops the day.”
- 18.818 s, tap Feedback: clock 05:00. Choices were I’d keep playing, Not sure, I’d stop here. There was no exact “I’m stopping” or “I’m confused” choice.
- 20.712 s, tap I’d stop here: the control became pressed. I typed nothing and did not press Submit. No receipt exists.

The pressed state added no new concept. |

## 4. Engagement and leaving

Best moment: 10.322 s, “I miss you, Nuran.” “I understand he misses her.” This was an understandable personal situation.

Worst moment: same screen, “You can wait without choosing a suggestion.” I had not found the suggestions.

One want_quit: 10.322 s, CONFUSED, at the second reread. It did not pass because I stopped. No three-minute pulse was due. No hard-but-kept-going moment; I did not look for an easier setting. The menu and feedback were required inspection after play stopped.

## 5. Clarity findings

- DL-20: “On Eid, the festival at its end, he will act without your advice.” The meanings of Eid and Ramadan were nearby, but I reread the time relationship and the change in my role. Transcript 0.479 s.
- DL-5: “You can wait without choosing a suggestion.” I could not tell where the suggestions were or what Speak would do. This describes my understanding before opening Speak, not a claim that suggestions were absent. Transcript 10.322 s.
- DL-3: The running clock and fasting end time made me wonder whether I must hurry. I had not learned which events needed my action. Transcript 10.322 s.
- DL-13: Local Feedback inspection worked. I chose “I’d stop here”; no note or verdict was submitted to a live endpoint.

No screenshot references are available. I did not establish a visual defect.

## 6. English, dates, layout, and direct action

DL-20: English allowed me to begin. Ramadan was defined as the fasting month and Eid as the festival at its end. I reread the Eid sentence. On-request explanations were NOT TESTED; Information was unopened.

DL-21: NOT TESTED. Mon 11 Mar, Ramadan 1, and fasting until 18:46 were visible, but I did not compare a deadline with today or cross a month/year boundary.

DL-22, DL-30, DL-31: NOT TESTED — not established by this text harness session. No screenshot was viewed and no order/answer sheet was opened. The required alternate viewport check was not reached because play stopped before five minutes.

DL-32: NOT TESTED as a blocked waiting stretch. I did not spend a measured stretch wanting to act while forced to watch. The initial loading state changed after my three-second wait.

DL-33: NOT TESTED. Speak was visible; I did not press it, so I cannot say whether speaking was direct or opened a form.

The game clock advanced between requests. I did not establish how the harness handles time while no request is active. This short session provides no evidence about missed events or live pacing.

## 7. Stop and return

Stopped in the first home/table scene at 10.322 s, CONFUSED, after the second sentence needing rereading. I would close it, rather than leave it running. I would not return voluntarily from this exposure. Local feedback inspection ended with I’d stop here pressed at 20.712 s. No game ending reached, no receipt, no telemetry verification, no export.

## 8. Changes I would want

1. “Show me the first suggestion when you tell me I can choose one.”
2. “Say separately when Eid comes and what happens to my advice then.”
3. “Tell me whether I need to hurry while I read.”

Cleanup: no workers, browser, server, worktree, branch, or screenshots created by this tester. The supplied driver/browser belong to the coordinator; coordinator was told they are no longer needed. No repository was touched, so there is no applicable git worktree list.

# Rosa Alvarez — Warren, free arm

## 1. Assignment and limits

- **Who and when:** Rosa Alvarez, 62, a busy reader whose second language is English. First visit to Warren; I have never read its source.
- **Round:** hf3-warren-r1, arm free, paired session hf3-warren-r1-rosa-alvarez.
- **Earlier diary:** my frozen free-branch diary (Voice R1, free arm) is the only history. No one in Warren reminded me of anyone, so there is no recognition to record.
- **Game:** URL https://warren.adamwhite.work, title "The Warren". No build number shown.
- **Device and access:** 360×690 touch, light theme. Local player driver at 127.0.0.1:19606, one `curl` POST at a time.
- **Time limits:** floor 0, ceiling 25 real minutes.
- **Timings (driver clock):** first screen 0.542 s; play stopped at 20.612 s; menu and Feedback check ended at 28.114 s.
- **What I did not change:** consent, the "Recording" setting and the "New rules" checkbox were all left at their defaults.
- **What I did not send:** no note, no name and no Submit. No export. No player id was shown.

## 2. First minute

I quit before 60 s, so these are my final observations at 20.6 s, not a 60-second checkpoint.

| Item                       | Result                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| DL-1 A reason to act       | PASS — "Dig a shelter where the village can survive attacks by riders." That is clear.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| DL-2 What can I touch      | PASS for text — Begin, the tools (look, chalk, rub out, furnish ▾, bell), the speeds Slow/Steady/Fast, zoom, Journal ▸, The village. I tapped Begin, chalk and the stone. How the buttons look: NOT TESTED — not established by this harness, apart from one screenshot. |
| DL-3 Urgency and selection | PASS — the instruction line drew me first. Chalk showed as selected (pressed). Nothing felt urgent; "Time slows for new text" reassured me.                                                                                                                              |
| DL-4 Why the limit         | FAIL — "Nobody can reach that stone yet." I tapped right beside the cellar, as I was told. Only after I opened the menu did a line say "Mark a path to them from the cellar".                                                                                            |
| DL-5 Readable situation    | PASS — "A dashed outline marks where people can dig. Each person chooses when to work."                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| DL-6 A choice soon         | PASS — a choice was available at 0.5 s (Begin). First input at 3.5 s. First real choice (chalk) at 5.8 s.                                                                                                                                                                |
| DL-7 Learn by doing        | FAIL — my one chalk tap was refused. What I learned ("open ground") did not match what I had been told ("beside the cellar"). Help (i) was not used. One choice was reached and the rest were NOT TESTED.                                                                |

## 3. Verbatim diary

- **0.542 s, title screen:** "I read the checkbox sentence twice: what is a hill here, and what is warned damage to furnishings? I leave it as it is and begin." This was **Reread 1**: "New rules for this new hill: sleep and shelter outcomes, limited wild food, and warned damage to furnishings. Existing hills keep their rules."
- **3.483 s, tap Begin.** Clock showed Dawn · 19 Aug · year 1. Prompt: "Choose chalk, then drag across stone beside the cellar."
- **5.797 s, tap chalk:** "It tells me plainly: choose chalk, then drag. I choose chalk." The answer was "A dashed outline marks where people can dig. Each person chooses when to work."
- **9.316 s, screenshot `current.png`:** "Where is the cellar?" A small box with a vertical shaft above it.
- **13.903 s, tapped the stone right of the box (235, 229):** "The cellar is the small box. I cannot drag with this, so I tap the stone just to its right." Clock showed Morning · 19 Aug · year 1. A Reading panel opened: "Nobody can reach that stone yet. Mark a path to it with chalk from open ground so people can dig toward it."
- **19.462 s, want_quit, CONFUSED:** "I read it twice … It told me to mark beside the cellar, I did, and now nobody can reach it? What is open ground, and where does my path start? That is my second reread." This was **Reread 2**: "Mark a path to it with chalk from open ground so people can dig toward it."
- **20.612 s, stop, CONFUSED:** "I did what the first line said and the game said nobody can reach it. I do not know where open ground is. I stop here."
- **23.103 s, Game menu (inspection after I stopped):** "The game is paused while the menu is open." The instruction line had changed to "The chalk marks are beyond the dug space. Mark a path to them from the cellar with chalk."
- **25.45 s, Feedback:** the choices were I would keep playing / I am not sure / I would stop here. There was no "I'm stopping" and no "I'm confused".
- **28.114 s, tap I would stop here:** it shows as pressed. I typed nothing and did not press Submit.

## 4. Engagement and leaving

- **Best moment:** 5.8 s — the chalk instruction was short and plain, and choosing chalk worked.
- **Worst moment:** 13.9 s — I followed the instruction and was told nobody could reach the stone.
- **want_quit:** 19.462 s, CONFUSED. It did not pass, because I stopped.
- **Pulses:** none; no pulse was due before I stopped.
- **Hard, but kept going:** none.

## 5. Clarity failures

- **DL-20:** I reread the "New rules for this new hill…" sentence. "Hill" and "warned damage to furnishings" are not explained (title screen, 0.5 s).
- **DL-4 / DL-7:** "Choose chalk, then drag across stone beside the cellar." I marked beside the cellar and got "Nobody can reach that stone yet… from open ground." "Open ground" is not explained. The clearer line ("from the cellar") appeared only later, when I opened the menu. This is from the transcript at 13.9 s and 23.1 s.
- **Possible input limit, not a game defect:** the driver has no drag, so I used a single tap. My tap may have landed outside the spot the game meant. I cannot tell that from the game.

## 6. English, dates, layout, direct action

- **DL-20:** I can act in English. "Open ground" and "hill" had no meaning nearby. I did not open Information (i), so explanations on request are NOT TESTED.
- **DL-21:** NOT TESTED. "19 Aug · year 1" is readable, but I met no deadline.
- **DL-22:** partly seen in `current.png` at 9.3 s. The instruction, the hill and the tools all fit on screen without scrolling. The rest is NOT TESTED.
- **DL-30:** none seen in `current.png`. Other screens are NOT TESTED.
- **DL-31:** NOT TESTED; no order or answer sheet appeared.
- **DL-32:** NOT TESTED; I never had to watch while wanting to act.
- **DL-33:** I marked digging on the hill itself, not through a form. The Reading panel opened after my tap.
- **Viewport check:** the second viewport is unmet; I stopped before 5 minutes.

## 7. Stop

- **Where and why:** I stopped on the first hill screen at 20.6 s, CONFUSED, after my second reread.
- **First want_quit:** it did not pass.
- **Return:** I would not come back on my own. I might return if someone showed me where to start the path.
- **Feedback:** "I would stop here" is pressed but not submitted. No receipt exists.

## 8. Changes I would want

1. "When you say 'beside the cellar', make that tap work, or show me where the path must start."
2. "Tell me what 'open ground' is the first time you say it."
3. "Explain 'hill' and 'furnishings' on the first screen in plain words."

The browser is no longer needed; the coordinator owns the driver. I created no worktrees, servers or extra browsers, and touched no repository, so there is no `git worktree list` to show.

# Rosa Alvarez: Warren playtest (hf3-warren-r1, continued arm)

I played past the 15-minute floor and stopped at 925.9 s (about 15.4 minutes), reason **BORED**. I could not save `report.md`: I have no file-write tool, and each Bash call may contain only curl. This response is the full report for the coordinator to save.

## 1. Session details

- **Persona:** Rosa Alvarez, 62, retired school administrator from Puebla. Busy second-language reader.
- **Exposure:** first visit to Warren; I did not read the game's source.
- **Round, arm, paired session id:** hf3-warren-r1, continued, hf3-warren-r1-rosa-alvarez.
- **Pre-game diary:** my frozen free-branch diary from Voice (a different game). It contains no Warren history. This session is an alternative exposure, not a continuation.
- **URL:** https://warren.adamwhite.work. The visible title was "The Warren". No build number was shown.
- **Device:** 360×690 touch/light. I briefly switched to 1280×800 between 294 s and 300 s for the required check.
- **Access:** player driver at http://127.0.0.1:19607/, one curl POST per call.
- **Time limits:** floor 15 minutes, ceiling 25 minutes.
- **Player id:** none shown in the ordinary player UI.
- **Export:** none downloaded.
- **Screenshots:** `current.png`, `current-wide.png`, and the driver's own `first-minute.png`. That is three in total.
- **Harness limits:**
  - The harness has no drag operation. Every "drag" instruction in the game was done with single taps, one square at a time.
  - The game clock kept running between my requests.
  - Waits were real waits of 20–30 s.

## 2. First minute

My `first_minute` note was logged at 66.9 s, which is late; the checkpoint was due at 60 s. The original note says: "Reason to act: riders will attack, the village needs a shelter, and the line at top tells me the next job… Tappable: look, chalk, rub out, furnish, bell, Slow/Steady/Fast, plus/minus, Journal, the village. I could not drag; one tap of chalk worked when next to the wall, too far gave a reading that nobody can reach it… Chalk is selected. Slow is selected… First choice at about 5 s (chalk), first useful mark at 27 s. Two rereads already, before one minute. I do not know what the jars and little cups at the top mean."

| Item                       | Result                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| -------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| DL-1 A reason to act       | **PASS.** "Dig a shelter where the village can survive attacks by riders," plus the top line "Choose chalk, then drag across stone beside the cellar."                                                                                                            |
| DL-2 What can I touch      | **PASS** for the tool buttons. The hill itself is tappable, but the squares are tiny. I did not know the jar and cup icons at the top meant anything, and nothing explained them. The visual look of the controls was checked on screenshot `current.png` (11 s). |
| DL-3 Urgency and selection | **PASS.** The top instruction line drew me first. "chalk" and "Slow" showed as pressed. Nothing felt urgent in the first minute.                                                                                                                                  |
| DL-4 Why the limit         | **FAIL.** I tapped stone "beside the cellar" and got "Nobody can reach that stone yet…" with "The chalk marks are beyond the dug space." I could not tell how far "beside" meant. Tapping directly against the wall freed it.                                     |
| DL-5 Readable situation    | **PASS.** "The dig is marked. If dug, this space would have about six sleeping places." Then "Meryem broke through the first chalked wall… coughing dust."                                                                                                        |
| DL-6 A choice soon         | **PASS.** The first choice (chalk) was available at 5.3 s and I took it at 17.5 s, before any long text.                                                                                                                                                          |
| DL-7 Learn by doing        | **PASS.** Chalking, waiting, and then seeing Meryem dig taught me that people do the work, not me. I used no help in the first minute. Choices reached: chalk, plus 2 hill taps.                                                                                  |

## 3. Verbatim diary

Times are driver elapsed seconds; each line is input → what I saw → my reaction.
- **0.6, opening screen:** a checkbox reading "New rules for this new hill… Existing hills keep their rules." "What is a hill here? Do I need to tick it? **Reread 1.** I leave it as it is."
- **5.3, Begin** → Dawn · 19 Aug · year 1, the hill view.
- **17.5, chalk** → "A dashed outline marks where people can dig."
- **19.8, tap the stone right of the cellar** → "Nobody can reach that stone yet…" with the top line "The chalk marks are beyond the dug space. Mark a path to them from the cellar with chalk."
- **24.1, want_quit CONFUSED:** "**Second reread**… I touched beside the cellar, as it said. Beyond? I thought I was beside. I would leave here by my rule. I continue because I set the time aside."
- **27.4, tap against the wall** → "The dig is marked… about six sleeping places." "Good! Six sleeping places."
- **62.2, after waiting** → "Meryem broke through…" and "The harvest needs more room below. Mark open ground as a store for the jars."
- **71–76, furnish → store, tap the cellar floor** → "The store is marked." "Yes, store. Clear words."
- **78.5, The village** → a list of six people by household. "Meryem and Maryam, two almost same names, I must be careful… ordinary people eating at home."
- **84.9, beds** → 3 of 6 circles filled: "Riders reach these beds from the entrance."
- **99.2, chalk below the room** → the panel was titled "Plan preview". "Did it mark it or only show me? I cannot tell."
- **124.5, Journal** → "Kian… still needs to care for his sister Ada." "I like this diary… Ordinary people."
- **128.5, Information** → help panel "Chalk and marks". "Help is clear, short… But nothing says when riders come."
- **142.0** → "The water jars are dry. People need a well or water carried below." "A well? I did not see well in furnish."
- **179.4, bell, by mistake** → "No smoke or riders are in sight. The call lasts six game hours."
- **211.1, Journal** → "They looked at the bell, then at you." "**I laughed.** Yes, it was me." The same journal also said "A lamp beside a stone wall lets them dig there."
- **218.3, lamp** → "The lamp is hanging here now."
- **253.6:** "Night 24 Aug then Dawn 24 Aug, the night comes before the dawn on the same day."
- **261.9, door placed in the shaft** → "The door is marked." "I worked it out myself. Proud."
- **290.9** → "Saliha rolled it once to test it." Now there were nine circles. "Who is Saliha?… more people came and nobody told me."
- **294–300, layout check (requested, not spontaneous)** → described in section 6.
- **312, tap "furnish ▾"** → error: the button was now named "door ▾". "It takes the name of what I chose last."
- **364.6, beds in the deep part** → 6 of 9 places. "'False alarms can weaken trust.' Ah, so my bell mistake cost something."
- **398.3, beds on the left** → "There is space for everyone." "All nine circles full! Very satisfying, like a seating plan for a family party."
- **439, Steady**: "so the quiet days go faster."
- **474.9, want_quit BORED:** "Everybody has a bed, the journal repeats the same lines, Steady feels no faster than Slow."
- **475.9, Fast** → **507.9:** "Time is paused for your choice. Smoke rises on the ridge." "Smoke! Now it is real… Ring the bell."
- **514–520, Slow, then The village, then Master Yusuf** → "74… He is exposed above ground during the raid." His line: "This is good enough for me." The panel also said "You are the keeper and hold the chalk and bell." I read twice: "**Ask to compare the old map with the line where work must stop today**". "What old map? What line? I only want to say: please come down."
- **545:** I waited 20 s and the clock stayed frozen, because the village list was still open.
- **570** → "Riders searched the entrances and looked in… No one was struck. They may return with more riders." The journal said that each person "could still be seen… would have been caught". "My door did nothing; nobody shut it."
- **584, screenshot `current.png`:** the riders' message covered the top of the hill and the + button.
- **663** → "The scouts rode off north… Next time they may strike."
- **670.7, look at a red square** → "Unbuilt plan… cannot be reached." "I think this is my very first wrong tap; the game said nobody can reach it, but it kept the mark anyway."
- **687** → 12 places. **723:** opened the menu. "No easier mode, no bigger text."
- **790.9** → "The Duman family came up the road with a donkey."
- **871** → 15 places for 13 people.
- **905** → "The Tuncer family came…" and I was 2 beds short again.
- **919–923, Feedback** → pressed "I would stop here". I typed nothing and did not Submit.
- **925.9, stop BORED:** "I understood the loop: chalk, wait, beds, a new family… one tap at a time on very small squares, and I could not make anyone shut the door. I would not leave it running. Maybe I come back one day for the raid, but not soon."

### Concepts on every inspected screen

Existing hills keep their rules." | I did not know what a "hill" was, or whether I had to tick the box.              |

Mark a path to them from the cellar with chalk."                                                      | I had tapped "beside" the cellar as told.                                        |

Nearly every screen added more than one new concept, which counts as a clarity finding under the protocol. The first two screens were the heaviest, with 14 and 16 new concepts.

## 4. Engagement

- **Best moment:** 398 s, "There is space for everyone." All nine circles were full. The 211 s line "They looked at the bell, then at you." was a close second.
- **Worst moment:** 518–570 s. Riders were coming, and old Yusuf "rests in the shade" with "This is good enough for me." I had no way to ask him to come down, and the door I built was never shut.
- **want_quit log:**
  - 24.1 s, CONFUSED: the second reread. It passed once a tap succeeded at 27 s.
  - 474.9 s, BORED: the quiet stretch. It passed when the smoke appeared at 508 s, then came back.
- **Pulses:**

| Time  | Engagement (1–5) | Why                                    |
| ----- | ---------------: | -------------------------------------- |
| 172 s |                3 | Liked the people; water dry, tiny map. |
| 357 s |                4 | Learning by doing.                     |
| 545 s |                4 | Smoke, worried about Yusuf.            |
| 720 s |                3 | Mostly watching.                       |
| 910 s |                2 | The loop felt like homework.           |
- **Hard, but kept going:** the squares were tiny and I kept missing them; the long top message covered the map; and there is no drag in this tool. At 723 s I looked in the menu for bigger text or an easier mode and found none. I stayed because I wanted to see the next raid and I cared about the families.

## 5. Clarity failures

- **DL-4 / DL-5 (19.8 s):** "The chalk marks are beyond the dug space." I did not know what distance "beside" meant. The refused mark also stayed on the map as an "Unbuilt plan", which I only found at 671 s.
- **DL-5 (99 s):** a panel titled "Plan preview" appeared and I could not tell whether my mark was placed.
- **DL-20 (518–520 s):** "Ask to compare the old map with the line where work must stop today". The old map and the line were not introduced anywhere I saw. Separately, the line "You are the keeper…" first appeared at about 520 s, more than 8 minutes in.
- **DL-3 (291 s):** three people arrived and the circle count changed. On the phone the only story line showed the door news, and the "Çoban family" arrival was visible only in the wide layout.
- **Silent stale state:**
  - The line under the hill said "Meryem… coughing dust" from 20 Aug until 23 Aug.
  - The top line went back to the opening cellar message at 670 s.
  - At 545 s I waited while time was paused, because the village list was still open, and nothing obvious told me.
- **Mechanics I could not resolve:** I could not make anyone shut the stone door. "Can choose" was the only explanation offered. This is about what I could tell, not a claim that no rule exists.
- **DL-13:** Feedback opened locally and worked. There was no "I'm stopping" or "I'm confused" choice, so I pressed "I would stop here". I typed no note and did not press Submit, so nothing was sent.

## 6. English, dates, layout, hands-on

- **DL-20: PARTIAL.** Most English was plain and I could act in it.
  - Main terms (chalk, store, beds, lamp, door) were explained on first use in the furnish menu.
  - "Hill" on the title screen and "old map/line" were not explained.
  - The help answered my question about the circles but not "when do riders come".
- **DL-21: PASS for reading dates.**
  - English dates crossed a month boundary without trouble ("31 Aug" to "1 Sep").
  - "Riders may arrive in half a day or less" was clear.
  - Quirk: the clock showed "Night · 24 Aug" before "Dawn · 24 Aug", and "Night · 11 Sep" before "Morning · 11 Sep". I had to work out that "Night" means the early hours.
  - No deadline was ever given to compare against today.
- **DL-22: PARTIAL, from screenshots.**
  - The immediate reply (top line, reading panel) was on screen at 360×690 without scrolling.
  - The hill itself is small, and the bottom half of the phone screen was empty (`current.png`).
- **DL-30: FAIL.**
  - At 584 s (`current.png`) the four-line riders message covered the top of the hill and the + zoom button.
  - The story line was cut off: "They were checking the village. No one was…" and "There were 4 people in the family.…".
  - In the wide layout (`current-wide.png`) the Tamar line was cut at "next time" at the bottom of the side list, and the hill area showed only empty paper, with no hill visible even after zooming in.
- **DL-31: NOT TESTED.** No order or answer sheet exists. With the village list open, the people count and date stayed visible.
- **DL-32:** the longest stretch of only watching was 401–508 s, then again 720–790 s. I wanted to make people safe and to move the story on. During the raid (514–663 s) I wanted to tell Yusuf to come below and could not.
- **DL-33: on the thing itself.** Digging, beds, lamp and door were all placed by tapping the hill. No form opened. Speaking to a person happens through their panel, which only offered adviser questions.

## 7. Stop

- **Where:** 925.9 s, after the Tuncer family arrived, at 11 Sep · year 1.
- **Reason:** BORED.
- **First want_quit:** passed after one tap.
- **Leave running?** No. I closed it rather than leaving it running.
- **Return voluntarily?** Possibly, much later, to see a real raid. Not soon.
- **Browser:** the coordinator can close it; I no longer need it.

## 8. Changes I would want

1. "Let me ask people to come below, or tell me plainly why they shut or do not shut the door."
2. "Make the squares bigger on my phone, and keep the message from covering the hill."
3. "Tell me when new people arrive, and say once at the start that I am the keeper."

**Cleanup:** I created no worktrees, branches, servers or workers. The three screenshots are within the limit, so none were deleted. I touched no repository, so `git worktree list` does not apply.

## 1. Session

Rosa Alvarez, 62, retired school administrator from Puebla. First visit to Valley; target source unread. Round **hf3-valley-r1**, arm **free**, paired session **hf3-valley-r1-rosa-alvarez**.

My supplied history was Voice free, Warren free, Warren return free, and Commonstead free. I recognized nobody here.

URL: https://valley.adamwhite.work. Device: **360×690 touch/light**, through the supplied curl driver. I pressed **Resume** on the prepared title page. I left speed, consent, and recording unchanged.

Floor 0; ceiling 25 real minutes. First screen **0.111 s**; stop **78.305 s**; final local note **108.910 s**. Release commit is for the coordinator to add.

## 2. First impressions

At **11.518 s**, I understood feeding people and earning trust, but “the landowner’s due” needed another reading.

At **50.587 s**, the grain payment had clarified that phrase. The large red harvest button gave me a clear first action. The small map names were harder to read.

## 3. Verbatim

These are all my original recorded reactions. Full raw input lines remain in **wall-clock.jsonl**.

**11.518 s — note**

> I understand keeping people fed and earning trust. Reread 1: “Bring in the landowner’s due, keep the valley fed, and earn its trust.” I read it again because I do not know what the landowner’s due means: money or grain? New concepts, 15: steward, landowner’s due, feeding the valley, earning trust, three-year term, elders remembering, people refusing and remembering my answer, grain wages, voluntary contributions, winter loans, inspections, pass journeys, family arrangements, road agreement, provisions and rest between walks. I leave the checkboxes alone. I am curious about the people.

**17.010 s — Resume**

> I open the prepared term.

**50.587 s — Call the hamlet to cut the landowner’s barley; input clock 18 July · year 1 · cloud**

> Now I see his payment is grain. Rain in two days is clear, and the large red button tells me what to do. I can read this phone screen; the little map names are small. New concepts, 12: residence, households on a map, barley harvest before rain, 384 grain measures, 15 September payment, former steward leaving at night, empty house above the mill, overseer, granary, rider counting gifts as missing, announcer carrying requests, ledger.

**56.917 s — the Kavak house; input clock 20 July · year 1 · storm**

> It is already the rain day. I was reading, and I may have been too slow. Nobody has answered yet. I go to the first house for a reply. New concepts, 3: visiting houses for replies, came/delayed/refused totals, call open until 26 July.

**64.230 s — first_minute; 20 July · year 1 · storm**

> I called for the barley and opened the first house, but it has no reply. Reread 2: “The current call is still open; another call for this work opens when it expires on 26 July, year one.” I have to untangle which call opens when. I thought the work had to happen before today’s rain. New concepts, 4: house About, visits take walking and sitting time, another call after expiry, sending grain.

**70.570 s — want_quit, CONFUSED**

> That is my second sentence needing another reading. I want to stop. I do not understand why I am waiting until 26 July when the rain is here now.

**78.305 s — stop, CONFUSED**

> I wanted to help bring in the barley. I made the call, but now the rain is here and the first house has no answer. I stop after my second reread. I would close it, not leave it running. I would return if someone explained the first call and how much time I have to read; I would not choose to return alone another evening.

**108.910 s — note after stopping**

> After stopping, I found both stopping and confused topics in Feedback. I chose stopping. It filled the note itself; I typed nothing and did not submit. The menu says time stops and progress saves. New concepts, 3: music setting, recovery volume saved in this browser, Lab privacy link. Feedback is familiar; the player-ID explanation is 1 new concept. I do not know what recovery volume means, but I did not reread it. The browser is no longer needed.

## 4. Moments

The clear harvest instruction interested me. I understood why barley should be cut before rain.

After my call, the visible date was **20 July**, with **0 came, 0 delayed, 0 refused**. Opening Kavak showed **“There is no new reply from this house.”** I had not visited the household.

My one quit wish was **70.570 s, CONFUSED**. It did not pass; I stopped without further gameplay. No three-minute pulse was due. The first-minute note was recorded at **64.230 s**, rather than exactly 60 seconds.

My notes counted **38 new concepts**.

## 5. What confused me or became clear

“Due” became clearer when I saw the payment expressed in grain measures.

The second reread concerned the current call expiring and another call opening. I could not connect that explanation with cutting barley before the rain already shown on the screen.

I wondered whether reading had made me too slow. That was my uncertainty, not an established missed-event outcome. I did not learn what a visit would reveal or whether the harvest could still succeed.

## 6. Actual device use

I viewed **current-phone.png**, captured at **19.961 s**, at **360×690**. The instruction and large red call button were readable. The map names were small. The notebook continued beneath the bottom action area.

Normal controls tapped: **Resume**, **Call the hamlet to cut the landowner’s barley**, **the Kavak house**, then, after stopping, **Menu**, **Feedback**, and **I’m stopping**.

**1280×800 was unseen and unused.** I stopped before five minutes, so the alternate viewport check remained unmet. I did not view the automatic first-minute artifact.

One Feedback command failed locally with `zsh:1: unmatched "`. Corrected shell quoting operated the control. This was not a driver block.

The date advanced during play. I did not establish whether time freezes between driver requests. I made no speed change and used no skip.

## 7. Stop and return

Stop: **78.305 s — CONFUSED**, at Kavak’s household panel, **20 July · year 1 · storm**.

> I wanted to help bring in the barley. I made the call, but now the rain is here and the first house has no answer. I stop after my second reread. I would close it, not leave it running. I would return if someone explained the first call and how much time I have to read; I would not choose to return alone another evening.

This was a natural free-arm stop. I would close it. I would not leave it running or choose another evening alone. My willingness to return depends on someone explaining the first call and reading time.

**I’m stopping** became pressed at **96.666 s** and automatically filled the note field. I typed nothing and did not submit. No production receipt exists.

## 8. Changes I would want

1. “Explain the first call and when I should expect an answer.”
2. “Tell me how much time I have to read before the rain.”
3. “Say ‘grain payment’ when that is what ‘due’ means.”

The supplied browser is no longer needed. I created no workers, browsers, servers, or worktrees and touched no repository. No repository worktree listing applies. The coordinator saves this report.

```text
I stopped at 453.8 s (about 7½ minutes) because I was **FRUSTRATED**. I did not write report.md, because every shell command could only be `curl`. Please save this response as the report. The browser is no longer needed.

# Rosa Alvarez — Valley, return test, free arm

## 1. Session
- **Who:** Rosa Alvarez, 62, retired school administrator from Puebla. I read English as a second language and I am busy.
- **Exposure:** a declared return. My only Valley history is my own R1 free entry. My other history is Voice R1 free, Warren free and Warren return free, Commonstead free, and Voice R2 free.
- **Round and arm:** hf3-valley-r2, arm free, paired session hf3-valley-r2-rosa-alvarez.
- **Game:** https://valley.adamwhite.work, title "The Valley". The coordinator adds the release commit.
- **Device:** 1280×800, keyboard/mouse, light theme, through the curl driver at [local transport address omitted].
- **What I pressed:** Resume on the prepared spoken-answer edition. I did not touch the checkboxes, speed, consent or Recording.
- **Time (driver clock):** open at 0.115 s, Resume at 5.7 s, stop at 453.8 s. The Feedback check ended at 465.0 s.
- **Game clock:** 18 July, year 1, 07:00 to 13:30. Rain is due 20 July.
- **Recognition:** I recognised nobody from other games.

## 2. First impressions
- **4.6 s, title:** I knew the title page from last time, and I knew the landowner's due is grain. The new spoken-answer line made me read it twice. That was reread 1 (quoted in section 3).
- **13.4 s:** "This time the line says it plainly: 'replies are available at the houses.' And 'Time moves slowly while you read' — that answers my question from last time about being too slow. Good."
- **65.4 s, first_minute (late by 5 s):** "Much better than last time. I called, visited Kavak, and five people came. Zehra will come after her own barley; I understood her at once. Only one reread so far, on the title. But now I waited twenty seconds and the clock still says 08:00 and '80 game minutes left here'. I am waiting, but for what?"

## 3. Verbatim
These are my original log words. The raw JSONL is kept separately.
- **4.6 s:** "I know this title from last time; the landowner's due is grain, I remember. But there is a new line about a spoken answer. Reread 1: 'This uses the original grain rules and local people, instead of the other editions below.' Original rules compared to what? Which editions? I leave the boxes as they were set for me. New concepts, 3: spoken answer during visits, editions, original grain rules. I press Resume as I was told."
- **9.3 s, Call the hamlet, 07:00:** "Same morning as last time. I know this now; I call quickly so I am not too slow, and this time I will wait for answers instead of opening a house."
- **13.4 s:** "…The call is open until 24 July, but rain is 20 July; I still do not like that. New concepts, 3: reading view slows time, replies at the houses, Skip to next event. Kavak has a little circle; I will try it now, it is still 18 July."
- **18.4 s, Visit Kavak:** "Same words as last time: no new reply. I remember that call sentence, I do not need it again. Last time I stopped here. Now there is Visit; I think a reply comes only if I sit with them. I press Visit."
- **34.0 s, Say: I came to listen, 08:00:** "Five people came! 'We'll be there.' That is the answer I missed last time. Now I can say 'I came to listen' — that suits me. New concepts, 2: a spoken line during the visit, a dot on Çoban."
- **41.3 s:** "Zehra: 'Who will feed my family while I cut his barley?' I understand her completely. She will come after her own barley. I will not insist; she would remember it, and I would too. New concepts, 3: postponing, Zehra and her own harvest, hold to insist and being remembered. Clear sentences. I like this house."
- **65.4 s, first_minute:** quoted in section 2.
- **70.9 s, Skip, 08:00:** "Nothing is moving. The game offers Skip; I try it once." The game moved to 08:30, and 10 came.
- **108.8 s, ledger:** "The ledger is clear like a school register: less than 5 cut, about 360 still in the field, rain on 20 July. That worries me: ten people came but almost nothing is cut yet. Two days. New concepts, 6: threshing from 5 August, collecting households' share from 9 August, granary 30, years with too little paid, elders' gathering 16 December year three, prior volumes. No reread."
- **173.1 s, pulse 4:** "I am engaged: I understand the people and their own barley, and I know what to do now — call, visit, listen. Only worry: almost nothing cut and rain in two days."
- **231.4 s, after a wait at Tepe:** "I said it again, but there is no answer from them. Like with Halil last time: did they hear me? Does listening change anything?" The game then showed "Sit down."
- **234.7 s:** "'Sit down.' A small answer, but warm. I feel welcome."
- **239.2 s, Skip:** "Tepe still says 'after our own barley'. Listening was kind but changed nothing I can see. Zehra's answer now says 'I cannot do it now' — did my visit make her colder, or is it the same?"
- **272.5 s:** "Skip went grey and nothing changed. I thought that was a button that moves the day."
- **287.2 s:** "On my big screen the replies are in large letters, good for me. The map names are small, and the residence and Danyal and Visitors labels sit on top of each other. The bottom row of houses is cut off at the left: I see 'avako'. The big red Skip button looks like the main thing to do, but it did nothing before. The long list of the same sentence is tiring."
- **303.9 s, Send grain:** "Oh! It sent the grain at once. I thought it would ask me how much. I do not know how much I gave. I am not sorry for Zehra, but I wanted to choose." Then: "Granary went from 30 to 24: I gave six measures. That I can live with."
- **349.5 s, pulse 3:** "I like the people, but now it is mostly waiting, and the same sentence nine times. Nothing seems to change after my gift."
- **373.8 s, want_quit, FRUSTRATED:** "Skip went grey again and nothing moved; I am still 10 minutes from home. I know what I want — to get to the rain and see if the barley is saved — and the big red button does not take me there. I would like to leave. I give it one more half minute."
- **409.8 s:** "Yusuf of the Çoban house: 'Tell the landowner we are grateful. Or thank God for us instead.' Ha — he knows the grain came from me, not from the landowner. That made me smile. My wish to leave passes for now."
- **450.9 s, Information:** "The help is plain and kind… But it does not answer my question. Nothing says why Skip is grey or how long the rain will take for me. New concepts, 3… No reread."
- **453.8 s, stop, FRUSTRATED:** quoted in section 7.

My notes counted **22 new concepts** in this visit. Only **one** sentence needed a reread.

## 4. Moments
- **Best moments:**
  - "5 people came… 'We'll be there.'" This was the answer I never reached in R1.
  - Zehra's question about feeding her family.
  - Yusuf's joke after my gift.
- **What lost me:**
  - Skip twice turned grey with no visible change, at 239 s and 368 s.
  - The same "After I finish this task (reap our own barley)…" sentence was listed up to nine times.
  - Send grain gave grain at once, with no amount to choose.
- **want_quit:** at 373.8 s, FRUSTRATED. It passed at 409.8 s because of Yusuf, then came back, and I stopped at 453.8 s.
- **Pulses:** 4 at 173 s, and 3 at 349 s.

## 5. What confused me or became clear
- **Became clear:**
  - Replies wait at the houses, and a Visit brings them.
  - Reading slows time, and menus or help stop it.
  - The grain bar and the ledger.
- **Still unclear:**
  - Why Skip is sometimes grey.
  - How many real minutes are left before the rain. At this pace it looked like more than 30 minutes; that is my guess.
  - Whether "I came to listen" changed anything.
  - "Instead of the other editions below" on the title page.
- The game clock moved about 30 game minutes for every 25–30 real seconds of driver waiting.

## 6. Actual device use
- **current-wide.png (1280×800), seen at 281 s:**
  - The reply text was large.
  - The map names were small.
  - The labels for residence, Danyal and Visitors overlapped.
  - The household row was cut off at the left edge ("avako").
- **current-phone.png (360×690), seen at 292 s:** this was the requested layout check, not my own choice. I resized at 288 s, tapped the Dere house, then returned to 1280×800 at 295 s.
  - The map became a thin strip.
  - The status line was cut off ("20 game minutes left h…").
  - The household row was also cut off at the left.
- **Controls I tapped:** Resume, Call the hamlet…, the Kavak house, Visit (twice), Say: I came to listen (twice), the Çoban house, the Tepe house, the Dere house, Back to the notebook, The ledger ▸, Open the landowner's ledger, Skip to the next event (twice), Send grain, Information, Menu, Feedback, I'm stopping.
- **Driver:** one tap failed because "The ledger ▸" was not shown in the house view. The game reported this; it was not a driver block. I did not view the automatic first-minute.png.

## 7. Stop and return
- **Stop, 453.8 s, FRUSTRATED:** "This time I got much farther: five, then sixteen people came, Zehra told me her trouble, Yusuf thanked me with a joke, and the barley is being cut. I liked those people. But now I only wait. A day and a half until the rain, half an hour for every half minute, and the red Skip goes grey. I want to know if the barley is saved and I cannot get there tonight. I stop here. I would close it, not leave it running. I would come back another evening if I knew how long the waiting is, or if Skip took me to the rain."
- **Would I return voluntarily?** Yes, on a condition: if I knew how long the waiting is, or if Skip took me to the rain. That is more willing than after R1.
- **Leave it running?** No, I would close it.
- **Another evening?** Yes, on the same condition.
- **Feedback:** I'm stopping became pressed and filled the note box by itself. I typed nothing and did not submit.

## 8. Changes I would want
1. "Tell me why Skip is grey, or let it take me to the rain."
2. "Show one line per house instead of the same sentence nine times."
3. "Let me choose how much grain to send before it goes."

**Cleanup:** I made no workers, servers, browsers or worktrees, and I touched no repository, so there is no `git worktree list` to show. My screenshots are current-wide.png and current-phone.png, which is within the limit.
```

## 2026-10-30 Glance LIGHT first visit — selected FREE history

Both devices belong to this same evening, in the order in the original report. The computer restart did not cut this standing player. Actual build e97b91077d9d9f584bf31c8f3fd2ad495d4c5432/HF4.14.0. No continued arm exists. [Original report](../reviews/light-2026-10-30/glance/rosa/report.original.md.txt).

**Would I return voluntarily? No. I would close it and not come back on my own, on either device, and I would not leave it running. I might come back only if someone first explained, in plain words, what "dark corners" and "arrangements" are, and showed me one job to do.**

**Ranked asks**
1. Remove the long "Nerys / dark corners / access strip / outgoing post" paragraph from the first screen, or put it behind a button. It is optional, but it fills the first screen I have to act on.
2. Explain a job in one plain sentence. For example: "Pick how many turns to open the gate. More turns lower the water." Drop "How Dai expects to feel", "insist" and the "caught" rule from that first view.
3. Say whether I must hurry while I read. The clock ran on to 22:54 while I was reading on the desktop, and lightning came with no warning.

## 1. Session

Source: [complete own report](../reviews/light-2026-10-30/rope/rosa/report.md), exact producer original at [report.original.md.txt](../reviews/light-2026-10-30/rope/rosa/report.original.md.txt), original SHA256 `887f35acbb6730f4b76b83a98776a933a1a5018719a99e0f92a01ac813a632e3`. Fresh phone 360x690 and desktop 1280x800 automated project-test contexts; sequential device visits, the second a return the same evening, not paired arms. Both Feedback labels were offered; the tester used a label and Back with no note typed or submitted (per the packet README). The agent run-source marker was requested through labRunSource=agent; no production recording receipt was independently read.

**Director method notes (not the tester's words)**
- Her supplied own-history contained an old Warren continued-alternative body (lines 225–405 of the supplied history, preserved as rope/rosa/own-history.original.md.txt). This is a director filtering breach. Her declared partial Read ranges cover it; its effect is unknown.
- Her original input and report are unchanged. Her direct current-target observations are history-qualified: do not use them as clean cross-game continuity or recognition evidence ([history-qualification.json](../reviews/light-2026-10-30/rope/history-qualification.json)).
- The director viewed her phone frame (a-003): text under the selected-person action row is covered by the lower action dock, and two more controls sit in different action groups. The warning heading itself does not overlap the buttons in that frame; pointer-cause attribution is unproved.

**Readable producer report follows. Formatting may add blank lines; the linked producer original is exact.**

**Would you return? "I would come back another evening on the large screen to see the first mountain through, but only if the rope words are explained; I would not leave it running, and I would not keep playing it on the phone as it is."**

Ranked asks:
1. Explain the rope words (rope up, close up, "something I need for that is missing") in plain sentences. Do it at the moment they first matter.
2. On the phone, keep the buttons in one place. Give the two "more ▾" buttons different names. Stop the warning heading from pushing the buttons down.
3. Say what "A call needs attention" refers to. Say what Skip does and how far it jumps the clock.

## 1. Session

- Persona: Rosa Alvarez, 62, retired school administrator from Puebla. Second-language English reader.
- Exposure: first visit to The Rope. Round light-1030, first panel round, free stop (floor 0).
- Own history: read once, lines 1–502 of 1299 only. It holds my earlier Voice, Warren and Valley diaries. I did not read the rest, so I invent no memories from it.
- URL: https://rope.adamwhite.work/?labRunSource=agent
- Session A: 360×690 touch phone, light mode. Driver on port 21306. About 213 s.
- Session B: 1280×800 keyboard and mouse, light mode. Driver on port 21307. About 78 s.
- I recognised no one from earlier games.

## 2. First impressions

- **A, 2.5 s:** the title page was clear. I read "Five people will climb three mountains… They can say no." and the three mountain drawings. "New game" was one big red button. I knew what to do.
- **A, 5.4 s:** the game asked "When do you turn back?" with four choices. I understood it and picked 13:00. All five people answered at once, each in their own voice. That was warm and easy to read.
- **A, about 30 s:** I was confused. There were two controls, "lead on" and "climb on". The hint said "Ask them to join you. Tap Climb on, then ask." I could not tell which one actually moves them.
- **B, 0.9 s:** the first screenshot was nearly empty. It showed a frame, an empty bar and two blank panels, while the text list said the title page. I saw no title text in the picture.
- **B, 6 s:** the real game screen was much clearer than on the phone. It had speech bubbles with names and big buttons. I recognised the same game.

## 3. Verbatim diary, with elapsed seconds

**Session A (phone)**
- 5.4 s: "Clear: five people, three mountains, they can say no. I start."
- 6.8 s: "A turn-back time is a sensible question; I pick the middle one."
- 8.0 s: "They all agreed. I lead on."
- 29.6 s: "Walking now; I watch what happens."
- 31.5 s: "Two things named climb on and lead on. Unsure which asks them. I try climb on."
- 33.2 s: "Gentle feels right; they can say no."
- 34.7 s, note: "Everyone agreed. I now see two layers: ask the group, and my own lead on. A little confusing but the answers are warm and clear."
- 66.9 s, first_minute: "I understand: lead five people up, they may refuse, keep my promise to turn back. I have asked, they agreed, the clock moves. Calm and clear; the hints appear one at a time which I like."
- 99.0 s: "Slow; I use Skip."
- 116.6 s: "It says a call needs attention but I do not see what. Looking."
- 119.4 s: "I see the picture: I am ahead, they are below. I will stay with them so nobody is left."
- 186.0 s, pulse, engagement 3: "Pleasant and clear, people are named and kind. But I wait a lot, and Rope up and Skip are terms I do not fully know. Still curious about the steep part."
- 188.1 s: "I follow the hint: pick someone then Rope up."
- 191.3 s: "Two buttons called more; I tap the one by the people actions."
- 197.0 s: "The warning heading pushed the buttons down; I tap more by the speak row."
- 198.6 s: "Rope up is enabled; I try it."
- 199.9 s: "Nell is nearest to Tamar in the picture; I choose her."
- 204.7 s: "Reread 1: Something I need for that is missing. Nell is too far away. The first part is vague, the second explains. A Close up button is offered; I try it."
- 207.0 s, want_quit, CONFUSED: "Second sentence needing reread: Rope up, A partner may stop a fall if you slip, and Something I need for that is missing. Fixed by the Close up button, so it passed; I stop because I have seen the main loop and the rope idea is a lot of new words."
- 212.6 s, stop, CONFUSED: "I stop here. I liked the named people and that they may say no, but the rope part has many new words and two buttons called more." Game clock: Hollin Crag 09:07.

**Session B (desktop)**
- 3.0 s: "Same title as on my phone; I recognise it."
- 5.8 s: "The first desktop picture was an empty frame, odd. Now I choose the earlier turn time to be safe."
- 9.2 s: "Desktop is much easier to read: bubbles with names, big buttons. Leading on."
- 13.3 s: "I am curious what pressure does; I would normally ask gently, but it warns it can cost trust."
- 46.3 s: "Skip to move on."
- 68.3 s, first_minute: "Same game as my phone. I know the loop now: promise a time, ask, wait, skip. Desktop is far easier to read. Still unsure what A call needs attention refers to."
- 69.0 s: "I want to see what the Journal is."
- 71.3 s, note: "I like the journal. Nell promised her mother a call, Wren came because Oskar said she would not. Ordinary people. Crampons explained as spikes for ice. This is the best part for me."
- 77.9 s, stop, BORED: "I stop for the evening. Same game, easier to read on the large screen, and I like the journal. But I mostly waited and pressed Skip, and I do not yet know what the call needing attention is." Game clock: Hollin Crag 06:50.

## 4. Moments

- **What held me:**
  - Each person answers in their own words.
  - Refusal is possible.
  - The journal has ordinary details, such as Nell's promise to call her mother and Wren coming because Oskar said she wouldn't.
  - The phone picture of the climbers on the slope was lovely.
- **What lost me:**
  - Long waits that ended in pressing Skip.
  - The rope terms.
  - The phone buttons moving when the warning appeared.
- **Quit wishes:**
  - A, 207.0 s, CONFUSED. It passed once "Close up" worked, but I stopped anyway.
  - B ended BORED at 77.9 s. I never logged a separate want_quit there.
- **Pulses:** one, at 186.0 s on the phone, engagement 3. The desktop session ended before 180 s, so no pulse there.
- **First-minute notes:** logged at 66.9 s (A) and 68.3 s (B), after 60 s each time.
- **Pace:** I could not tell what Skip does or how far it jumps. In A it moved the clock from 05:37 to 06:49, then from 07:05 to 08:58. That was a jump of an hour or two. I did not know that would happen.

## 5. Confusion and clarity, from actual play

- Unclear: "lead on" versus "climb on". The hint "Tap Lead on to start walking… Ask them to join you" mixes the two.
- Unclear: "A call needs attention". I looked at the screen and could not find what it pointed to.
- Unclear: "Something I need for that is missing." This was Tamar's refusal when I asked her to rope up with Nell. The next sentence, "Nell is too far away to attach to the same rope", did explain it.
- Clear: the hints named the next thing to do, one at a time.
- Clear: "Close up: Tamar Vale + Nell" fixed the refusal at once.
- Clear: the weather warning gave an estimate, "not a deadline".
- Clear: "Insisting… can damage their trust in you".
- Clear: the journal explained crampons as "spikes for walking on ice".

Concept table, per screen I saw (counts are my own, not invented for unseen screens):

| Screen             | New ideas I met                                                                                                                                          | Count |
| ------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----- |
| Title              | five people, three mountains, they can say no, Journal, info, menu                                                                                       | 6     |
| Turn-back question | turn-back time, a promise they remember, Saving/Time paused                                                                                              | 3     |
| Hill screen        | speak to everyone, named people, climb on, rest, eat, wait, more, answers list, lead on, go down, stop, stay with them, time slows for reading, time bar | 14    |
| Steep ground hint  | rope up, partner, pick someone, more actions, take pack, share food, weather warning                                                                     | 7     |
| Refusal text       | refusal, delay, insist, trust damage, close up                                                                                                           | 5     |
| Menu               | feedback, full screen, recording, pace auto/slow/normal/fast, download run, open run file, title page, privacy, debug                                    | 10    |
| Feedback           | stopping, confused, keep playing, not sure, stop here, note, name, submit                                                                                | 8     |
| Journal (desktop)  | timed diary entries, Ezra's map, crampons                                                                                                                | 3     |

## 6. Device use

- **Screenshots viewed (3 phone, 2 desktop):**
  - `phone/a-001.png`: title page. The title, three mountain drawings and the red "New game" button were readable. A small bordered "We record and link play" notice sat over the bottom edge.
  - `phone/a-002.png`: the climb. A slope with stick figures, a time bar, and labelled buttons. It was readable but dense.
  - `phone/a-003.png`: the weather-warning heading overlapped the buttons and pushed them down, and a line of text was cut off beneath them.
  - `desktop/b-001.png`: taken right after open, almost empty. It showed an empty bar and blank panels, and no title text. I did not see whether it filled in later. I tapped "New game" from the text list.
  - `desktop/b-002.png`: a two-column layout with speech bubbles on the left and big buttons on the right. Easy to read. Unavailable buttons carried a dashed outline and a reason ("You are at the hut. Climb first.").
- **Phone controls used:** New game, turn-back choice, lead on, climb on, ask gently, Skip, Tamar Vale, rope up, Nell, Close up, Game menu, Feedback, I'm confused, Back. One tap by coordinates (320,494 and 320,553) for the "more" button, because two controls had the same name. The first coordinate tap hit the weather warning instead.
- **Desktop controls used:** New game, turn-back choice, lead on, climb on, urge, Skip, Journal, Back, Game menu, Feedback, I'm stopping. I used no keyboard keys.
- **Seen versus unseen:** the layout problems above are from screenshots. I did not see the rest of the second and third mountains.

## 7. Stop and return

- **Session A:** stop at 212.6 s, CONFUSED. My words: "I stop here. I liked the named people and that they may say no, but the rope part has many new words and two buttons called more."
- **Session B:** stop at 77.9 s, BORED. My words: "I stop for the evening. Same game, easier to read on the large screen, and I like the journal. But I mostly waited and pressed Skip, and I do not yet know what the call needing attention is."
- **Feedback:**
  - Both devices offered "I'm stopping" and "I'm confused" labels.
  - In A, I opened Game menu → Feedback and tapped "I'm confused".
  - In B, I tapped "I'm stopping".
  - Each choice filled the note box with its own label. I typed nothing, changed no consent or recording setting, and pressed no Submit. I closed Feedback with Back each time.
- **Would I return voluntarily?** I would come back another evening on the large screen to see the first mountain through, but only if the rope words are explained. I would not leave it running, because it needs me to answer the calls. I would not keep playing it on the phone as it is.

## 8. Changes I would want

1. Explain the rope words (rope up, close up, "something I need for that is missing") in plain sentences, at the moment they first matter.
2. On the phone, keep the buttons in one place. Give the two "more ▾" buttons different names. Stop the warning heading from pushing the buttons down.
3. Say what "A call needs attention" refers to. Say what Skip does and how far it jumps the clock.

## 9. Compared with my last visit

First visit. Nothing in my own history mentions this game.
