**Readable producer report follows. Formatting may add blank lines; the linked producer original is exact.**

# Hearsay: Rosa Alvarez report

**Would you return voluntarily?** "I would come back another evening on the big screen, but only if the game told me plainly what to do while the garden is empty and how to order the bread. I would not leave it running, and I would not come back on the phone if all I do there is wait."

**Ranked asks**
1. When everyone has left the garden, say what I can do now and when they will return. On the phone I waited about two minutes with nothing to do.
2. Say in one plain sentence how to get the bread and produce arranged by Day 4. Put it on the first screen, not only in the Garden record.
3. Make the "Talk about someone else" lists work, and show that it is a folded heading, not a button. On the desktop my choice in the "Who?" list timed out.

## 1. Session

- **Persona:** Rosa Alvarez, 62, retired school administrator from Puebla. Second-language English reader.
- **Exposure:** first target visit to Hearsay. I read all of own-history.md (lines 1–718) and the cleanup rule. The history holds my diaries from The Rope, The Glance, Good Hands, Voice, Watch, Pass, Valley, Commonstead and Left Hand. None mention Hearsay.
- **Round and arm:** light-1030, first panel round, free stop (floor 0, ceiling 25 minutes).
- **URL:** https://hearsay.adamwhite.work/?labRunSource=agent
- **Session A:** 360x690 touch phone, light mode, port 21406. About 196 s.
- **Session B:** 1280x800 keyboard and mouse, light mode, port 21407. About 31 s.
- I recognised no one from earlier games.

## 2. First impressions

- **A, 0.5 s:** the title was clear: I run a tea garden in Istanbul and want a paid workers' lunch on Day 13.
- **A, 2.7 s:** the screenshot showed a calm page with Begin, Feedback and Fullscreen buttons in a row.
- **A, 5.3 s:** after Begin there were eight named people and a long Day 13 line. The line said the bread, produce and cooking were not arranged. I understood it.
- **A, 11 s:** I liked that Rıza's secret was ordinary and kind: doorway photographs and a daughter.
- **B, 1.9 s:** the title page looked good on the big screen.
- **B, 7.6 s:** "Talk about someone else" looked like a button but was a folded heading.

## 3. Verbatim diary

These are the lines the driver recorded as reactions or logs, in order. Elapsed seconds are the driver's.

**Session A (phone)**
- 2.7 s: "Title is clear: I run a tea garden, goal is a paid lunch on Day 13."
- 7.4 s: "Eight people, Riza asks about a lane. I try the gentle option."
- 9.5 s: "A fifteen-minute talk; clear. I let it finish."
- 11.4 s: "A private secret about Riza. I keep it private and offer help. I like this."
- 13.2 s: "Nice, ordinary. Now I turn to Derya."
- 18.4 s: "Derya was hurt for reporting an error. Sad, ordinary. I help privately."
- 52.6 s: "Everyone left; benches empty. I wonder how I order bread. I open the record."
- 68.8 s: "Waiting is slow; I try Fast."
- 130.5 s: "Still empty benches. Fast is only about one game hour per half minute. I wait."
- 71.5 s, first_minute, engagement 3, Day 1 08:30: "I understand the goal: I run a tea garden, people tell me private things, I keep them private or repeat them, and I need lunch supplies agreed by Day 4. The record explains the goal. Text is long but plain. Now everyone has left and I only wait. I do not know how I order bread."
- 187.2 s, pulse, engagement 2, Day 1 10:26: "The two talks were warm and clear, but for two minutes I only wait on an empty garden. Nothing tells me what to do next or when people return."
- 189.2 s, want_quit, BORED, Day 1 10:26: "Empty garden for three minutes and no task I can do. I want to leave. I will check the menu and feedback first."
- 196.5 s, stop, BORED, Day 1 10:30, engagement 2: "I stop here. I liked the two quiet talks and the private choices. But the garden emptied and I only waited, and I do not know how to order the bread."

Disclosure: my first attempt at the first_minute log failed on shell quoting at about 66 s and sent nothing. I resent it plainly at 71.5 s, which is after 60 s. I also sent one invalid wait op ("kind") at 54 s by mistake. The driver said it was my invalid request, not a driver failure.

**Session B (desktop)**
- 3.9 s: "Second visit, big screen. Same title; I know the aim now."
- 5.8 s: "This time I want to see what Talk about someone else does." (The tap failed; it was a folded heading.)
- 7.6 s: "It is a folded heading, not a button; I did not know that from its look."
- 12.8 s: "I choose Osman and Honesty. I only ask what Riza has heard. I will not accuse anyone." (The select timed out.)
- 20.5 s: "The select control timed out twice. I try tapping the option instead." (The reply said Osman had left the page.)
- 23.7 s: "I cannot choose the person in the Talk about someone else list. I open the menu to stop."
- 30.7 s, stop, TOOL, Day 1 06:19, engagement 3: "I stop. The Who? list in Talk about someone else would not take my choice; it timed out twice and tapping the option also failed. I did not get to play on the big screen beyond this."

I logged no first_minute, pulse or want_quit on the desktop. The session lasted 31 s, so those checkpoints are untested there.

## 4. Moments

- **What held me:** Rıza and Derya's private stories. "Offer practical help and keep this private" gave a gentle, clear result: a walking route on public streets, and a request letter that "Hatice helps check". Money also rose from 400 to 540 lira by itself.
- **What lost me:** at 06:42 to 10:30 game time, everyone left and the benches were empty. The text said "There are no customers to serve." Nothing told me what to do or when they would return. On Fast the clock moved about one game hour per 30 real seconds. I never reached the part about supplies.
- **Quit wish:** A, 189 s, BORED. It did not pass. I stopped at 196 s.
- **Pulse:** one, at 187 s on the phone, engagement 2.
- **Desktop:** the select problem was probably a tool limit, not a game fault. I cannot tell which. The same "Who?" list showed as plain clickable options in the driver's list.

## 5. Confusion and clarity, from actual play

**Clear**
- The goal and the Day 13 line, once I opened the Garden record: "Agree on supplies on Day 4 and paid cooking on Day 8. You need at least four reservations."
- The conversation choices: the buttons said what they did, and there was a "Let the conversation finish" button.
- "Making a claim does not prove it. Something told to you in private stays private unless you repeat it." I understood this on first reading.

**Unclear**
- "Hatice has not noticed enough to describe Rıza's condition." I did not know what a "condition" was or how to notice more. I did not reread it, but I could not use it.
- "Garden record: what you heard" is folded, and the goal sentence about Day 4 and Day 8 sits only inside it. I found it by chance.
- What I should do when the garden is empty. The game did not say.
- On the desktop, "Talk about someone else" looks like a button but is a folded heading. Inside it are two lists and four buttons ("Make a damaging claim", "Withdraw your accusation") that I did not understand.
- "Pace: Automatic. The garden slows while you read." changed to "Automatic pace is available in the game menu" after I tapped Fast. I did not know what Automatic does.

Rereads: none required a second reading, so I did not reach my quit rule for rereading.

### Per-screen concept table

Counts are my own, from what I saw.

| Screen (what it showed, elapsed_s)      | Exact sentence(s) I had to reread | Concepts the screen asked me to learn                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Count |
| --------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----- |
| A: Title (0.5 s)                        | none                              | tea garden, workers' lunch, Day 13, How to play (folded), Begin, Feedback, Fullscreen, Recording checkbox, Other ways to begin, All games, People across games, Lab privacy, Debug                                                                                                                                                    | 13    |
| A: Garden at 06:00 (5.3 s)              | none                              | clock and day, Game menu, Day 13 lunch line (bread, produce, cooking, meals reserved), lira cash and debt, Pace Automatic, Pause/Slow/Normal/Fast, eight named people, the garden picture, "condition", Invite a quiet conversation, Press for private details, Talk about someone else, Pour tea without sharing news, Garden record | 16    |
| A: Conversation in progress (7.4 s)     | none                              | fifteen-minute conversation, Let it finish, Leave it                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | 3     |
| A: After the talk (9.5 s)               | none                              | About Rıza (folded), Offer practical help and keep this private, Keep this private, Repeat this at another table                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | 4     |
| A: Empty garden (50.7 s)                | none                              | people out and away, benches empty, Sit and talk again disabled                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | 3     |
| A: Garden record open (52.6 s)          | none (long, but plain)            | the day's list with the goal, deadlines Day 4 and Day 8, a hidden-looking invoice story with Osman, "the account from the wedding reception"                                                                                                                                                                                          | 4     |
| A: Game menu (189.7 s)                  | none                              | clock paused, Resume, Use automatic pace, Feedback, Fullscreen, New game, Download this run, Other ways to begin                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | 8     |
| A: Feedback (191.5 s) QUIT              | none                              | verdict group, "What is happening?" group, note, name, queued with play history, what is sent, Not now, Submit                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | 8     |
| B: Title (1.9 s)                        | none                              | same as phone                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | 13    |
| B: Garden at 06:00 (3.9 s)              | none                              | same as phone, no new items                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | 16    |
| B: Talk about someone else open (7.6 s) | none                              | Who? list, What? list (promises, honesty, kindness, generosity), Ask what they have heard, positive claim, damaging claim, withdraw accusation, a rule note                                                                                                                                                                           | 8     |

I quit on the phone at the Feedback screen after the empty garden. The two sentences nearest my quit were "The benches are empty. There are no customers to serve." and "Hatice has not noticed enough to describe Rıza's condition." I did not need to reread either, but neither told me what to do.

## 6. Device use

**Phone (360x690, touch)**
- **Screenshots viewed:**
  - `phone/a-001.png`: the title page. I saw the "Hearsay" heading, three short paragraphs, "How to play" folded, and the three buttons Begin, Feedback and Fullscreen. I also saw the Recording checkbox and a bordered recording notice at the bottom. Text was readable.
  - `phone/a-002.png`: the game at 10:05. I saw the top bar with "Hearsay Day 1 · 10:05" and a "Game menu" button, the Day 13 lunch line, the lira line, and Pause, Slow, Normal and Fast buttons with Fast highlighted. Below them was Derya's card, which was cut off at "Derya writes a specific request for an…". The garden picture and the people buttons were not on that first screen. I did not scroll to see them, so I never saw the picture.
- **Controls used:** Begin, Invite a quiet conversation, Let the conversation finish, Offer practical help and keep this private, a name button (Derya), Garden record, Fast, Game menu, Feedback, I'm stopping, Not now.
- **Trouble seen:** the phone page pushed the garden people below the first screen, so on a phone I never saw them in a picture. I also tried to tap "Dilan at the garden" at 50 s, but everyone had moved. The driver reported "the page has changed" and that was game state, not a tool failure.

**Desktop (1280x800)**
- **Screenshot viewed:** `desktop/b-001.png`: the title page. A centred column of text, large heading "Hearsay", the Begin, Feedback and Fullscreen buttons, the Recording checkbox, and a wide bordered notice at the bottom. It was easy to read. I took no screenshot of the game screen on the desktop, so I did not see its layout. I took two manual screenshots in total on phone and one on desktop.
- **Controls used:** Begin, Talk about someone else (tap by text), the "Who?" select (failed twice), a tap on the "Osman" option (failed), Game menu, Feedback, I'm confused, Not now.
- **Block:** the "Who?" select timed out twice ("locator.selectOption: Timeout 1500ms exceeded", marked "driver failure"). The one tap on the option also failed because Osman "left the page just before the tap". I treat this as a possible driver block and stopped as TOOL. I cannot say whether the game or the tool caused the timeout.
- **Unseen:** the desktop game layout, and anything after the first conversation.

## 7. Stop and return

- **Session A:** `stop`, BORED, at 196.5 s. My words: "I stop here. I liked the two quiet talks and the private choices. But the garden emptied and I only waited, and I do not know how to order the bread."
- **Session B:** `stop`, TOOL, at 30.7 s. My words: "I stop. The Who? list in Talk about someone else would not take my choice; it timed out twice and tapping the option also failed. I did not get to play on the big screen beyond this."
- **Feedback:** both devices offered "I'd keep playing", "Not sure", "I'd stop here", "I'm stopping" and "I'm confused". On the phone I chose "I'm stopping". On the desktop I chose "I'm confused". I typed nothing, changed no consent or recording setting, and pressed no Submit. I closed each with "Not now".
- **Would I return voluntarily?** Yes, but only on one condition: the game must tell me plainly what to do when everyone leaves the garden, and how to order the bread. I would come back another evening, on the big screen, to find out what happens to Rıza and Derya. I would not leave it running, because the garden empties and I just watch. I would not return on the phone to wait.

## 8. Changes I would want

1. When everyone has left, say what I can do and when they return. Example: "The garden is empty until 12:00. You can read the record or skip ahead."
2. Put the goal on the first screen in one sentence: "Agree on bread and vegetables by Day 4, and on cooking by Day 8." Now it appears only inside the folded Garden record.
3. Make "Talk about someone else" clearly a folded heading, and check that the "Who?" and "What?" lists accept a choice on the desktop.

## 9. Compared with my last visit

First visit.

## 2026-10-31 Stairwell LIGHT — free stop

Source: [complete own report](../reviews/light-2026-10-30/stairwell/rosa/report.md), exact producer original at [report.original.md.txt](../reviews/light-2026-10-30/stairwell/rosa/report.original.md.txt), original SHA256 `213c081e10e6aa03743b3917be5be3210416d06c61b73d884e39395eddb0bc53`. Build c2bfaae81f1b250a8c250075e00e71c421d8481b/HF4.14.0, dirty:false. Fresh phone 360x690 and desktop 1280x800 automated project-test contexts; sequential device visits, the second a return the same evening, not paired arms. Both Feedback labels were offered; the tester used a label and Back with no note typed or submitted (per the packet README). The agent run-source marker was requested through labRunSource=agent; no production recording receipt was independently read.

**Director method notes (not the tester's words)**
- First target visit. She reached one reread, not her two-reread gate; no forced continuation is inferred. Her first phone stop reason was invalid and rejected (192.227 s); TASTE at 196.236 s is the accepted stop.
- Two enabled controls named "as I heard it" caused an ambiguous driver tap; coordinates worked. This supports a semantic-label clarification, not functionally broken buttons.
- Her earlier Good Hands, Glance and Rope reports retain their historical input qualification; do not turn them into clean longitudinal recognition proof.

**Readable producer report follows. Formatting may add blank lines; the linked producer original is exact.**

**Would you return voluntarily?** I would come back another evening, on the big screen or the phone, to find out what the neighbours think of me and what the Sunday vote brings. I would come back only if the game tells me in plain words what "as I heard it" and "for certain" mean, and warns me before the unkind newspaper swap. I would not leave it running while I do something else, because the jobs wait for my taps.

**Ranked asks**
1. Explain **Tell…** in one plain sentence ("as I heard it" vs "for certain", and "Make something up…"). Give the two same-named buttons different names, for example "Hacer's news: as I heard it". On the phone, two buttons had the same name and I had to tap by point.
2. Say at the start, in one sentence each, how the Sunday vote works, what "trust" and "feelings" mean, and what the ₺ amounts are. Also say how far **Skip** jumps.
3. Mark mischief choices, such as **Swap newspapers**, with "may upset someone" before I tap. I did not know the swap was unkind until Hacer saw me.

---

## 1. Session

- **Persona:** Rosa Alvarez, the busy second-language reader.
- **Exposure:** first visit to this game.
- **Round and arm:** light-1030, first panel round, free stop (floor 0).
- **Own history:** `own-history.md`, 850 lines, read fully in two reads. It has nothing about this game. I used it only to recall my habits: deliberate taps, help read only when it answers a question, no assumed conventions.
- **URL:** https://stairwell.adamwhite.work/?labRunSource=agent
- **Devices and real time (driver elapsed_s to the stop log):**
  - Session A: 360x690 touch phone, light mode, driver port 21506. Stop log at 196.2 s.
  - Session B: 1280x800 keyboard and mouse, light mode, driver port 21507. Stop log at 192.8 s.
- **Both sessions started a new game.** I left recording at its default (on). I did not change consent or recording.

## 2. First impressions

**Phone**
- 3.6 s: the title page tells me I deliver supplies to five flats, with a Sunday vote on tea money. "Several ideas."
- 12 s: the game screen shows a building picture, orders and many tabs. I see "Go to the bakery".
- 13.8 s: "Clear job: bread for Sevim. I go."
- 24.9 s: a new button, Swap Hacer and Sevim newspapers. I did not know why it was there, so I ignored it.

**Desktop**

- 12.2 s: the order differs from the phone. Hacer is first, not Sevim.
- 16 to 20 s: the Orders tab is clear, with names and deadlines. The Residents tab shows in/out and minutes. I guessed the minutes are walking time, and the Orders list confirmed it.

## 4. Moments

**What held me**
- Every order is a plain job with a name, a place and a time.
- The neighbours are ordinary people with understandable lives. Tarık and the frozen ferry was the story I liked most. Hacer's shop-counter story was the second.
- Skip always brings another job, so I never felt stuck.
- The desktop Orders and Residents lists were clear at a glance.
- The feedback choices ("I'm stopping", "I'm confused") are plain.

**What lost me, a little**
- The two "as I heard it" buttons had the same name (phone).
- The Swap button gave no warning that it is unkind.
- Words I never saw explained: trust, claim, stamps, the ₺ tips, "what you have learned 1 of 16".

**Quit wishes.** I logged no `want_quit`. The rule needs two rereads plus the concept table. I reached one reread (desktop). I stopped by choice (TASTE: I had seen the loop). The wish never came from confusion or boredom.

**Pulses:** phone engagement 4 at 182.5 s; desktop engagement 4 at 184.8 s.

## 5. Confusion and clarity

**Clear**
- "Go to the bakery", "Go to flat 7", the Orders list, walking minutes, "due 08:15", "Time is paused".
- "Anything new?" is a plain question.
- "You don't know how Hacer feels about you" tells me the game hides this on purpose, and I understood it.

**Reread (1)**
- "A reply takes two minutes. They can choose to leave the subject." I reread it once and guessed. Hacer then did leave the subject ("I would rather leave it there"), so the sentence was true and just compressed.

**Not clear**
- **Tell…** with "as I heard it" and "for certain" (phone). I guessed it was how sure I sound.
- Two buttons with the same name.
- The Swap newspapers button and its cost.
- ₺5 as a tip.
- "Claim" and "what I heard" in the gossip text.
- "1 of 16" in the notebook.

**Concept-count table.** The list below shows each concept a screen asked me to learn, with the exact sentences that carried it.

| Game screen, 07:03  | Orders, walking minutes, due time, "you may feel tired", building view, tabs                                           | 6 to 7   |
| Conversation        | "Anything new?", Tell…, Sit together, "Will you support my raise on Sunday? Ask from Thursday at 07:00, face to face." | 4        |
| Tell…               | "as I heard it" vs "for certain"                                                                                       | 2        |
| Notebook            | "What you have learned 1 of 16", Stamps                                                                                | 2        |
| Desktop pulse count | orders, walking minutes, due time, tired, feelings/trust, tips, tell/sit, Sunday raise vote                            | about 8  |

Counts are my estimate. I did not reach the two-reread gate.

**Stop words**

- **Desktop:** "I stop the big screen here for the evening, not from trouble. I saw the same loop as on the phone, with a different first order and my swap trick. One sentence needed a reread; the quit gate of two rereads was not reached." Reason: TASTE.

**Would I return voluntarily?** Yes, another evening, on the big screen or the phone. I want to see what Hacer and Sevim think of the swap, whether Tarık's trust grows, and what happens on Sunday. I would come back only if Tell… is explained in plain words and the mischief button warns me first. **Readable producer report follows. Formatting may add blank lines; the linked producer original is exact.**

**Would you return voluntarily?** Yes, another evening, on the big screen or the phone, to see what Dilan's Tuesday and her Saturday look back bring. I would return only if the game tells me in plain words what "trust" does and what "She wants to do it later" means. I would not leave it running, because Dilan waits for my taps and the 64-second timer keeps going.

**Ranked asks**
1. Explain "Hold to insist. This costs trust." in one plain sentence: what trust is, where I can see it, and what I lose at zero.
2. Explain the three labels under each choice ("She is willing", "She would agree only if you insist", "She wants to do it later") in one place, before the first choice. The third label appeared only on the phone.
3. Say what "Skip ahead" does and how far it jumps. It moved the clock by 7 to 40 minutes.

# Two Voices (assigned as "Murmur"): Rosa Alvarez report

The page title and heading on both devices were "Two Voices", not "Murmur". I played it as given.

## 1. Session

- **Persona:** Rosa Alvarez, 62, busy second-language reader.
- **Exposure:** first visit to this game.
- **Round and arm:** light-1030, first panel round, free stop (floor 0).
- **Own history:** I read lines 1 to 865 of 1203 in two reads. The history has nothing about this game. Lines 866 to 1203 are unread, so I claim no memory from them. I used it for my habits only: deliberate taps, help only for a concrete question, quit at the second reread.
- **URL:** https://murmur.adamwhite.work/?labRunSource=agent
- **Phone:** 360x690 touch, light, port 21606. Stop logged at 191.4 s.
- **Desktop:** 1280x800, light, port 21607. Stop logged at 191.8 s.
- **Recording:** left on. I changed no consent. I typed no note and pressed no Submit.

## 2. First impressions

**Phone**
- 0.6 s: "Two Voices". I am a voice in Dilan's head, and I suggest what she does from Monday to Saturday.
- 3.0 s: the screenshot shows large readable text. Two checkboxes about a "third week" and an "outcomes week" mean nothing to me, so I ignored them.
- 11.3 s: a 58-second timer and three plain choices. Each choice has a line under it about how she feels.
- 23.2 s: she slept through two alarms because I had suggested sleep. I saw at once that my words matter.

**Desktop**
- 1.9 s: the same title, centred, with large text.
- 12.5 s: the same first choice. I insisted she get up.
- 33.2 s: "She was up before the second alarm." The Murat lateness scene never appeared. The day differed from the phone because of my choice.

## 3. Verbatim diary lines

These are the reactions I attached to ops, with the driver's elapsed seconds. Two log attempts failed on shell quoting (one on the phone pulse at 184 s). Both were resent plainly. Those two resends were not driver blocks.

**Phone**
- 3.0 s (shot a-001): "Title: I am a voice in Dilan head, suggest what she should do Mon to Sat. Four people named. Two checkboxes about third week and outcomes week I do not understand; I ignore them."
- 5.0 s (tap Start): "I start. The game says Two Voices, not Murmur; I just play."
- 12.8 s (tap Sleep for five more minutes): "A timer of 58 seconds. Plain choices. She is willing to sleep; I suggest that, gently."
- 25.2 s (tap Read the message without replying): "She slept through two alarms because I suggested sleep. I did not expect that to cost her so much. I see: my words matter."
- 46.4 s (hold Say you overslept): "I want her to be honest, so I hold to insist, as the game said. It costs trust, but telling the truth seems right."
- 64.4 s, first_minute, engagement 4, Mon 10:41: "I understand: I am a voice, I suggest what Dilan does, she answers in a short time. Insisting costs trust. Plain, kind small stories. Not clear: what trust does for me, and why the timer counts down. Waiting between events is slow."
- 66.8 s (hold Talk her through it): "Ece is nearly crying. A map alone seems cold; I insist on talking her through it."
- 103.8 s (hold "She found it, didn't she?"): "Murat mocks Ece, who was crying. I do not like it. I insist on defending her."
- 138.2 s (hold Drink some water): "Third coffee for a tired woman is not good. Water and a walk."
- 172.2 s (tap Text Mum): "A third wording now: She wants to do it later. I guess it means she prefers not now. I pick the text, a small kind step."
- 186.1 s, pulse, engagement 4, Mon 21:31: "I care about Dilan: tired, late, avoiding her mother. Sentences are plain and short, no reread yet. Odd: she ignored my text suggestion and chose to scroll. I cannot see what trust I have."
- 191.4 s, stop, TASTE, engagement 4, Mon 21:31: "I stop the phone here for the evening, not from trouble. I liked Dilan and her small ordinary troubles. No sentence needed a second reading. I saw the loop and want to see the big screen."

**Desktop**
- 1.9 s (shot b-001): "Second visit, big screen. Same title; I know the aim."
- 15.7 s (hold Get up): "On the phone she slept late. This time I insist she gets up, to see the difference."
- 36.6 s (hold Tell Aylin): "She got up on time because I insisted. Now I insist she promises Aylin to call Mum tonight."
- 66.2 s, first_minute, engagement 4, Monday 10:42: "Same game as the phone, easier to read. My insist made her get up on time, so the Murat late scene did not appear. So my words change the day. That is clear and I like it. Still unclear: what trust is for."
- 67.9 s (tap Send her the map location): "This time I choose the easy map. Watching what differs from the phone."
- 101.4 s (tap Laugh along): "I do not like laughing at Ece, but I want to see what happens when I just take the easy willing choice."
- 135.9 s (hold Drink some water): "I feel bad that I let her laugh at Ece. Now I try to help her tiredness."
- 170.6 s (hold Call Mum): "I promised Aylin tonight. I insist she calls Mum. Here the label says only if you insist; on the phone it said she wants to do it later. The wording changed between runs."
- 185.6 s, pulse, engagement 5, Monday 21:31: "I like this. Dilan called her Mum for twenty minutes and laughed. The neighbour roof joke made me smile. My insist choices changed the day compared with the phone. No sentence reread."
- 191.8 s, stop, TASTE, engagement 5, Monday 21:31: "I stop the big screen here for the evening, not from trouble. I saw that insisting changed her day compared with the phone, and the Mum call was warm. No reread. I stop because I have seen a full Monday."

## 4. Moments

**What held me**
- Dilan's small, ordinary troubles: oversleeping, avoiding her mother, a nervous new driver.
- The sentence "He said that before the rain, too." The neighbour's roof made me smile.
- Each choice shows a plain line about how she feels.
- Insisting visibly changed the day. On the desktop, a held "Get up" removed the whole late scene.

**What lost me, a little**
- The slow gaps. I waited 8 to 30 s for the next choice, and Skip ahead jumped the clock by an unknown amount.
- I could not see what "trust" is or where its level is shown.
- On the phone I insisted on Call Mum and tried Text Mum, and she still chose to scroll. I could not tell why.

**Quit wishes:** none. I never reached two rereads.
**Pulses:** phone 4 at 186.1 s; desktop 5 at 185.6 s.

## 5. Confusion and clarity

**Clear**
- "Tap a choice to suggest it."
- "Below each choice, you can see how she feels about it."
- "Hold to insist. This costs trust."
- The 06:40 to 21:31 story lines were plain and short.

**Not clear**
- What "trust" is, where I see it, and what happens when it runs out.
- The label "She wants to do it later". It appeared on the phone only, at 18:10 and 21:30, and I had to guess its meaning.
- The two title checkboxes, "Play a third week" and "Play the outcomes week", which I ignored.
- "Skip ahead": how far it jumps.
- "The pencil handwriting shows the other voice." I never saw pencil handwriting in the text. I read it once and moved on.
- On the phone, the choice order differed from the desktop's. The Murat late scene only appeared after I chose to sleep.

**Rereads:** none. The standing rule never triggered.

| Feedback      | "I'd keep playing / Not sure / I'd stop here", "I'm stopping / I'm confused"                                                                                                             | verdict, what happened, name or tester ID, what is sent, Submit = 5 (running 21)       |

# Hearsay retest: Rosa Alvarez

**Would you return voluntarily?** "I would come back another evening on the big screen, now that the lists work and the bread goal is on the first screen, but only if the game says why someone is 'busy' when they are sitting right there. I would not leave it running, and on the phone I would not return only to wait."

**Ranked asks**
1. Say why a person is "busy" when they are at the garden, and when they will be free.
2. Say when people come back after the garden empties, not only what I can do meanwhile.
3. Keep the long "Pace: Automatic…" sentence short. It repeats on every screen and I skip it.

1. Rewrite the cook sentences I could not follow. This covers "I will explain the wait. I am leaving the other tables for three minutes…", the "Insist / Hold it to insist" help line, and the status "in the bathroom" beside that sentence.
2. Show early whether ₺5,200 is reachable (still not seen).
3. Warn about Dilek's meal break in the rush as well as Selim's. After that, make the "Nobody will work at the grill while Selim eats" line match the break I chose.

## 4. Moments

**Held me**

- Tables being served without trouble: 4 tables and ₺160 by 18:18 on the phone, and ₺360 after nine tables by 19:36.
- The desktop two-column layout. Cook choices on the right, kitchen picture on the left, and the whole screen read without scrolling.

It appears when he leaves, and the status beside it says "in the bathroom" or "outside". The words do not match the picture, and I did not know who he explains to.
- "Tap a job to ask. Hold it to insist, or open "Insist on a job" for tap and keyboard controls." I do not know what insist or hold means here.
- On desktop, the plan dialog still said "Nobody will work at the grill while Selim eats" after I moved the break to 21:00. I could not tell whether that referred to now or later.
- On the phone, Selim was "eating" at 19:48 although I had moved his break to 21:00. I do not know why. I am guessing it was an earlier or different meal.

- Why Dilek's planned meal break at 18:35 in the rush leaves the cold side empty. The planning screen warned me only about Selim's break.
- Why Dilek also said "Waiting for a new answer from Dilek" when I changed only Selim.

**Phone (360x690, touch).** Screenshots viewed: a-001 (title page) and a-002 (kitchen at 18:18). I also took a-003 (Feedback) and read it so I could find the "I'm confused" button.

But I earned only 200 lira by 19:32, three tables wait for an empty grill, and Selim says he will explain the wait while the screen says in the bathroom. I do not understand what he does. I would close it, not leave it running. I stop."
- **Feedback:** both buttons were available. I chose "I'm confused" on the phone and "I'm stopping" on desktop, each by screen position. Each became pressed and the note box showed that label's text. I typed nothing and pressed no Submit. I closed with Back to the menu each time.

**Would you return voluntarily?** I would come back another evening, on the big screen, if the game told me early whether ₺5,200 is reachable and if the strange cook sentences ("I will explain the wait…", "Insist", "Hold it to insist") were written plainly. I would close it, not leave it running, because the game moves on by itself while I read and I would not know what I missed. I would not choose the phone again unless the cook menu got shorter.

## 8. Changes I would want

1. Rewrite the sentences that confused me. "I will explain the wait. I am leaving the other tables for three minutes." should say who he talks to. Make the status ("in the bathroom") match the quote. Replace "Insist" and "Hold it to insist" with plain words.
2. Show early whether ₺5,200 is reachable. A line such as "You have ₺360. You need ₺5,200 by Saturday." would do.
3. Warn about Dilek's meal break in the rush as well as Selim's. Also make the plan dialog line "Nobody will work at the grill while Selim eats" match the break I chose.

## 9. Compared with my last visit

- **Do not let the starting plan leave the grill empty, or tell me before I open the doors (ask 1):** met for Selim. The planning screen now says "Selim's meal break (18:10–18:30) leaves the grill empty when the early table arrives at 18:12. Move that break or choose a cook to cover it." I fixed it in one tap. I met a similar problem for Dilek: her planned 18:35 break in the rush left the cold side empty (Session B at 18:41), with no warning on the planning screen. So this is partly met. The dialog also still said "Nobody will work at the grill while Selim eats" after I moved the break (Session B).
- **Show early whether ₺5,200 is reachable (ask 2):** not met. I saw only "₺160 … ₺5,200 due Saturday" and a bar. I saw no forecast.
- **Keep the planning text short; the long repeated "I agree to that…" quotes (ask 3):** not met. The planning screen is still four paragraphs, and "I agree to that. I will work at the grill until 21:00…" still appears in opening notes and again in the log. Related to my older asks, the "Earlier conversations" and "Opening notes" lists are still long.
- **From my earlier Well and Warren asks:** I did not play those games this time, so not seen. The closest idea (tell me what changed after I act) applies here. When I moved the break, the plan said "Waiting for a new answer" for both cooks, then "agreed" after I asked. That helped, but I did not understand why Dilek was also waiting.
- **Make it clear when a cook dialog is closed (from my last Pass report):** partly met. After a request the buttons became disabled with "They heard you. You are waiting for their answer." That was clear. I did not test the close button.
- **New blockers from this visit:** the "Insist / Hold it to insist" help line and Selim's "I will explain the wait" quote. Each cost me a reread, and together they ended both sessions. Also, Selim ate at 19:48 when I had moved his break to 21:00, and I could not tell why.

**Readable view of the producer's report follows. Formatting may adjust blank lines, table spacing or list markers; the words are the producer's, and the linked producer original is exact.**

**Would you return voluntarily?** "I would come back another evening, probably on the big screen, to see what happens to Özlem and the Kaya family. I would not leave it running; I would close it."

**Ranked asks**
1. After a placement, tell me what to do while I wait. Both sessions ended on "Next: A family is due to call. Wait or meet someone on your bench."

3. Keep "I'm stopping" and "I'm confused" in Feedback on both devices. Both were there this time.

---

## 1. Session

- **Persona:** Rosa Alvarez, 62, retired school administrator from Puebla. She reads English as a second language and is busy. Rule: stop after the second sentence she must reread.
- **Exposure:** retest. I read all of own-history.md in chunks (lines 1–468, to the end). It covers Well, Warren, Pass, Good Hands and the start of Glance. The Glance section was cut off at the file end, so I have no memory beyond it.
- **Round and arm:** light-1031-goodhands-retest1, free stop (floor 0). Build 2e67317e / 4.8.1.
- **URL:** https://goodhands.adamwhite.work/?labRunSource=agent
- **Session A:** phone 360x690 touch, light, port 22706. About 70 s of driver time at the stop log.
- **Session B:** desktop 1280x800, light, port 22707. About 43 s at the stop log.
- **Not reached:** I stopped before 60 s on desktop and I never reached 180 s. I logged no first_minute on the phone (see Section 3), and no pulse in either session.

## 2. First impressions

- **A, 0.5 s:** the same title as last time. The aim, the family and the rent were clear on first reading.
- **A, 9.4 s:** the office showed ₺3,000 and "Rent ₺5,000". I do not remember a starting cash figure from before, so I cannot say if that is new.
- **A, 31.5 s:** the crying question gave a clear "I take a breath before I answer", and the reading said "I believe".
- **B, 7.4 s:** the same office, with the big picture on the left and the card on the right.

## 3. Verbatim diary (driver seconds)

**Session A (phone)**
- 4.6 s, Open the office: "Same title as before; I know the aim."
- 12.3 s, Özlem Koç: "I remember Özlem from last time; I start with her. Cash is 3,000 now, not what I remember."
- 23.3 s, still interviewing: "Honest, simple."
- 35.7 s, wait on the bench: "Patient answer is clear: she takes a breath. The reading now says Özlem said this, I believe. I understand it."
- 41.7 s, Negotiate: "New: the card now says why: only Özlem says so, nobody confirmed, enough to believe. That is what I asked for."
- 44.9 s, patient: "The fee paragraph now has numbers: 2,250 now, 6,750 after trial. That was my ask. Still a long paragraph but I read it once."
- 51.0 s, agreement: "The same wage and hours are said three times on this screen. Too much repeating, but numbers are clear."
- 56.5 s, shot a-002: "Rent is covered already at under a minute. Placed card shows one sentence now, not five."
- 59.8 s, want_quit BORED (engagement 3): "I placed one nanny and the rent is covered in under a minute. The Next line is whole now and the buttons sit at the bottom. But now it only says wait; I would stop for tonight."
- 70.1 s, stop BORED (engagement 3): "I placed Ozlem and the rent is covered at 09:35. Reasons are explained now and the fee has numbers. Now I only wait for the next family call, so I stop for tonight. Feedback: I pressed the stopping label, typed nothing, no Submit."

Two log calls failed because of an apostrophe in the shell quoting. I resent the stop line with plainer wording, and it worked. The apostrophe is missing from the logged line: it says "the stopping label", not "I'm stopping".

**Session B (desktop)**
- 2.6 s, Open the office: "Same title, big screen. I know the aim."
- 10.6 s, Şebnem Eren: "This time I try Şebnem, the one I doubted before, to see whether the reading explains itself."
- 35.1 s, shot b-002: "New: the card says why she stays unsure: not clear enough. That is what I asked for."
- 43.1 s, stop BORED (engagement 3): "On the big screen I understood everything without a reread. The card now says why Sebnem stays unsure. I have placed nobody here and I would rather stop for tonight. Feedback: I pressed the stopping label, typed nothing, no Submit."

## 4. Moments

- **Held me:**
  - The card now says why a reading is "believe" or "unsure". Özlem's own answer was "enough for her to believe it". Şebnem's was "not clear enough, so she stays unsure". This is what I asked for last time.
  - The agreement screen gave the fee in numbers: ₺2,250 now, ₺6,750 after the trial. Cash then rose to ₺5,250.
  - The phone layout had a whole "Next" line and the action buttons in a bar at the bottom.
- **Lost me:**
  - After the placement I only had "Wait or meet someone on your bench". Nothing told me what was worth doing.
  - The agreement screen says the wage and hours three times.
- **Quit wishes:** A at 59.8 s, BORED. It did not pass, so I stopped. B ended in BORED at 43 s with no earlier want_quit line.
- **Pulses:** none. The 180 s mark was never reached.

## 5. Confusion and clarity

- **Rereads:** none in either session.
- **Clear:** the aim, the question list, "Nermin's reading" with its reason, and the numbers in the fee paragraph. The "Wage ₺8,500" note on the card also helped.
- **Unclear:**
  - "Nobody else has confirmed it" sits above "This is enough for her to believe it". It is a long, compact block, but I understood it on the first reading.
  - It surprised me that ₺5,250 passed the ₺5,000 rent in under a minute. I did not know what else was worth doing.

# The Rope: Rosa Alvarez, return-visit report (round light-1030-retest1)

**Would I return voluntarily?** Yes, on another evening, if someone asked me to try it, because the writing about the people is kind and clear. No, I would not leave it running, and I would not come back tonight. I mostly waited and pressed Skip, and I have seen the whole loop twice.

**Ranked asks**
1. Keep Skip on the screen. Say how far it jumps ("Skip to 08:00" or "Skip to the next event: about 1 hour").
2. Rewrite the sentence "You also stop at a place on the path when nobody behind can hear you" in plain words. Say which place, and why they cannot hear me.
3. During the long waits, write what I am waiting for and what I can do now. For example: "Waiting. You can open the Journal, or press Skip."

---

## 1. Session

- **Persona and exposure:** Rosa Alvarez, 62. Retired school administrator from Puebla, reads English as a second language, busy. RETURNING player, same-standing retest. Round light-1030-retest1, free-stop arm (floor 0, ceiling 20 min).
- **Own history supplied:** own-history.md, 1921 lines. I read all of it in chunks, through the End marker. It holds my earlier Rope report (line 505) and my reports on other games.
- **Qualification kept:** In the earlier Rope visit, the CONT input worked differently from a real person's. That qualification stands. The earlier method notes still apply.
- **Not supplied:** other testers' reports, game source, cast notes. I also did not read brief.md, dispatch.json, history-source-map.json, processes.json, tester.stderr or transcript.jsonl.
- **URL:** https://rope.adamwhite.work/?labRunSource=agent. Fresh game each session. There is no matched seed.
- **Devices:**
  - Session A, 360x690 touch phone, light mode, about 196 s real time.
  - Session B, 1280x800 keyboard and mouse, light mode, about 194 s real time.
- Neither session reached the 20-minute ceiling. I stopped both myself.

## 2. First impressions

**Session A (phone)**
- 1.9 s: I saw the title page. "Same title as last time; I know it."
- 11.99 s: the lead-on hint now says that lead on moves only me and climb on asks the others. That was my question last time.
- 65.7 s: no reread yet. I understood the loop.

**Session B (desktop)**
- 1.5 s: the first picture is now a filled title page. Last time it was an empty frame.
- 9.2 s: first reread, about the place "where nobody behind can hear you".
- 74.4 s: the Journal is warm and clear. The clock was stuck at 05:03.

But I have seen the same loop twice tonight: ask, lead on, wait for the clock, and Skip comes and goes. I would close it, not leave it running. In Feedback I pressed I am stopping, typed nothing, no Submit."

## 4. Moments

**What held me**
- The Journal. Small stories about the people: Tamar checking boots, Nell and her mother's call, Bilal and the stove.
- Words explained as they appear: "crampons, spikes for walking on ice".
- Rope up worked the first time (phone), and disabled buttons gave reasons.
- The waiting line on the phone: "You are waiting for Tamar Vale, Oskar, Nell, Bilal and Wren to catch up."

**What lost me**
- Long waits with little to do.
- Skip appearing and disappearing, with no stated size of jump.
- The desktop clock stuck at 05:03 ("Time slows for reading") until I tapped lead on a second time.

**Quit wishes**
- I logged no `want_quit`. I reached only one reread (desktop), so my second-reread rule never fired.
- I stopped both sessions as BORED, after about 3 minutes each.

**Pulses:** both at engagement 3 (A at 184.1 s, B at 182.6 s).

## 5. Confusion and clarity (actual play only)

**Clear this time**
- The lead on / climb on difference, which was my main question last time.
- The Skip label.
- The two "more" buttons having different names.
- "after a refusal" on insist.

**Confusing**
- "You also stop at a place on the path when nobody behind can hear you." I did not understand what place, or why they could not hear me.
- Skip is not always on the screen.
- On desktop, the clock stayed at 05:03 after I tapped lead on. I do not know whether my first tap worked or the second one did.
- On desktop, the path runs under the right panel, so I could not see where it goes.
- After "I'm stopping", the note box filled itself with "I'm stopping". I typed nothing.

**Not reached:** the "All 5 answers" panel, the "i" Information button, Close up, and any second mountain.

## 6. Device use

**Screenshots viewed**

## 7. Stop and return

**Stop words**
- Phone: "I stop the phone here for tonight. No sentence needed a reread this time, and the rope worked first try. But I mostly waited and pressed Skip, and I have seen the loop. I would close it, not leave it running." Reason: BORED.
- Desktop: "I stop the desktop here. Only one sentence needed a reread, so I did not hit my quit rule. But I have seen the same loop twice tonight: ask, lead on, wait for the clock, and Skip comes and goes. I would close it, not leave it running." Reason: BORED.

**Would I return voluntarily?**
Yes, on another evening, if the game showed me something new. I want the second mountain, or a person asking me for something I did not expect. I came back tonight because I was asked, and the writing about the people is why I would come again. I would not leave the game running while I did something else. The waiting makes me want to press things, and I cannot tell whether a press did anything. I would not come back tonight. I would play on my phone before the desktop, because the phone screen showed everything at once.

## 8. Changes I would want

1. Keep Skip on screen and say how far it goes.
2. Rewrite the "nobody behind can hear you" sentence in plain words.
3. During waits, say what I am waiting for and what I can do now.

## 9. Compared with my last visit

**Earlier asks**
1. **Plain rope words.** Partly met. Rope up worked at once with Bilal on the phone, and disabled buttons said why ("Walk to her first."). I did not reach "Close up" or "Something I need for that is missing", so I cannot say about those.

3. **"A call needs attention" and Skip.** Partly met. The phone now says plainly "You are waiting for Tamar Vale, Oskar, Nell, Bilal and Wren to catch up." Skip is now labelled "Skip to the next event". I did not see "A call needs attention" this time. Skip still comes and goes, and it does not say how far it jumps.

**New this time that would stop me returning**
- Skip is missing on both devices at times, with no reason given.
- On desktop, the clock stayed at 05:03 until I tapped lead on a second time, and I could not tell whether the first tap had worked.
- One sentence in the lead-on hint still needs a reread: "You also stop at a place on the path when nobody behind can hear you."
- The note box fills with "I'm stopping" when I press that verdict. I typed nothing, but a reader might think they must send it.

## 2026-10-31 goodhands-retest2 — free stop

Source: [readable report view](../reviews/light-2026-10-30/goodhands-retest2/rosa/report.md), [exact producer original](../reviews/light-2026-10-30/goodhands-retest2/rosa/report.original.md.txt), original SHA256 `e5cab6578b172514e0ff8dd4f9258e11d510e7c9feff32b7dc12b795455b0248`. Method qualification: [rosa](../reviews/light-2026-10-30/goodhands-retest2/rosa/director-method-qualification.json).

**Readable view of the producer report follows. The linked original preserves the exact bytes.**

**Would you return voluntarily?** I would come back another evening on the big screen, but only if the long paragraph that explains how Nermin weighs an answer is shortened. I would not leave it running; I would close it.

**Ranked asks**
1. Shorten the "Nermin received…, She weighs each account by how strongly it spoke…" paragraph under each quality. The one-line reading above it already says it, for example "I doubt that the worker is patient when someone needs time."
2. Replace the six "You have not asked yet." lines on a new applicant card with one line.
3. After a placement, say plainly what to do while waiting. On the phone the Next line hinted at a check-in call, but the screen still read as nothing to do.

## 1. Session

- **Persona and round:** Rosa Alvarez, 62, returning player. Round light-1031-goodhands-retest2, free-stop arm (floor 0, ceiling 20 minutes). Standing role: busy second-language reader.
- **History read:** own-history.md was read in full, 2122 lines, to the last line "End of supplied own history". Nothing was left unread.
- **Method qualifications kept:**
  - Warren was played with single taps rather than drags.
  - The Hearsay desktop TOOL stop was not shown to be a game fault.
  - The earlier history-read limits (Hearsay retest1 read 826 of 1419; Good Hands retest1 read 468 of 1682) are director metadata.
  - The Warren alternative body is absent, and no alternative visits are merged.
- **Earlier Good Hands reports:** the first report and the retest1 report are both in that history.
- **URL:** https://goodhands.adamwhite.work/?labRunSource=agent
- **Session A:** 360x690 touch phone, light mode, driver port 22806. It ran 205 s of driver time. The ceiling was not reached.
- **Session B:** 1280x800 keyboard and mouse, light mode, driver port 22807. It ran 87 s. The ceiling was not reached.
- **Both sessions were fresh games.** In both, I stopped by choice.
- **Git:** I touched no repo, so there is no `git worktree list` to show.

## 2. First impressions

**Session A, phone**
- At 3 s: "I know this title from before. Aim, family, rent: clear."
- At 13 s: I recognised Özlem from before. My confidence is fairly high, because I saw her name on the bench and remembered the carer story.
- At 68 s: a new Next line suggested checking in by phone on a placed worker.
- At 88 s I recognised the Kaya family when they called. My confidence is fairly high.

**Session B, desktop**
- At 1.9 s: the same title and the same three names (Gülce, Şebnem, Özlem). The type is comfortable on the big screen.
- This time the family was the Arslans, not the Kaya family. It was a fresh game, so I expected that.

## 3. Verbatim diary

I used no apostrophes in my own words. Where text appears below, it is as logged.

**Session A, phone (elapsed seconds)**
- 3.0: I know this title from before. Aim, family, rent: clear.
- 13: I remember Özlem from before…
- 47.0 (note): Reread 1. The wording is not preserved verbatim. It was the long evidence sentence on the applicant card.
- 63.0 (first_minute): the wording was not preserved verbatim.
- 68: Rent is paid in about a minute. New: the Next line says I can check in by phone on a placed worker at Tuesday 18:20. That is a hint about what to do while waiting.
- 73: Last time I only waited. Let me look at Families…
- 80: Nothing else to do. I skip to the next family call.
- 88: Kaya family, I remember them. I answer.
- 92: A carer for someone who throws a plate. Patient again. I promise to look.
- 97: Back to the bench. Gülce has waited three hours; I should see her.
- 100.7: The little half-circle is new. I guess it means they are tired of waiting. Guessing. I open Gülce.
- 156.6: Gulce card is mostly empty: You have not asked yet, six times. Clear. I interview her.
- 168.3 (note): Gulce says she speaks angrily. Nermin reading says why: I doubt she is patient. Clear, one reading. Not for the Kaya family. I send her home. (game clock Mon 2 Nov 11:52)
- 179.6 (want_quit, CONFUSED): Second reread. The paragraph: She weighs each account by how strongly it spoke and by how much she trusted the speaker when she heard it, for one quality of one person at a time. Too long, too abstract. I only needed: she said she is angry, so I doubt she is patient. I would stop reading here, but the short reading above it was clear, so I keep going a little. (11:52)
- 182.1 (pulse, engagement 3): I still follow the story, but the long reading paragraph tires me. One more candidate, then I check Feedback and stop. (11:52)
- 202.2: Both I am stopping and I am confused are here on the phone. I press confused.
- 205.2 (stop, CONFUSED): I stop here for tonight on the phone. The long weighing paragraph made me reread twice. The story is still clear and I would come back. (11:58)

**Session B, desktop**
- 1.9: Same title as the phone. Aim, family, rent: clear on the big screen.
- 4.7: I open the office. This is a fresh game, so I expect new people.
- 13.1: Same three names as the phone: Gulce, Sebnem, Ozlem. I remember Ozlem and choose her first. (08:37)
- 23.5: A call from the family. I answer.
- 27.4: The family needs a patient person. Same need as before. I say I am still interviewing.
- 41.97: Ozlem answer is unsure, not a yes or no. Short reading, one sentence, clear. I ask hours.
- 65.3 (first_minute): I understand the aim and the steps: interview, patient, wage 8,500 against family 9,000. The big screen text is easy to read. Ozlem left after four questions and told me when to call back. Clear. No reread yet on this screen. (09:28)
- 74.6 (want_quit, CONFUSED): I read the long weighing paragraph twice on the desktop: She weighs each account by how strongly it spoke and by how much she trusted the speaker when she heard it. Too many ideas in one paragraph. Same paragraph as on the phone, so it is not a screen size problem. I skip it and keep playing. (09:29)
- 83.6: Both I am stopping and I am confused are here on the desktop too. I press stopping.
- 86.8 (stop, CONFUSED): I stop on the desktop after about 85 seconds. The long weighing paragraph again tires me and I have seen the same loop as on the phone. I did not reach a placement here. (09:34)

One logging attempt on the desktop failed at the shell and sent nothing. I retyped it shorter, and that is the want_quit line above. I wrote the 47 s and 63 s phone entries from memory, so their wording is not exact.

## 4. Moments

**What held me**
- The family request is clear, and it asks for patience in plain words.
- Applicants answer in ordinary speech and then leave when their time is up. Özlem left after four questions and told me when to call back.
- "Nermin's reading" gives a short reason in one sentence. That was my first-report ask, and it is met.
- The Kaya call on the phone brought back a story I remembered.

**What lost me**
- The long weighing paragraph that appears on every card after an interview.
- The six "You have not asked yet." lines on an unasked card.

**Quit wishes**
- **Phone:** I logged one want_quit (CONFUSED) at 179.6 s. It passed briefly while I used Feedback, then I stopped at 205 s.
- **Desktop:** I logged one want_quit (CONFUSED) at 74.6 s. It did not pass, and I stopped at 86.8 s.

**Pulses**
- **Phone:** one pulse, engagement 3 at 182 s.
- **Desktop:** no pulse. I stopped at 87 s, so the 180 s check was not tested.

## 5. Confusion and clarity

**Clear**
- The rent, the family and the wage figures are clear.
- The interview shows "4 questions remain" and "Each question takes 8–12 minutes".
- The one-line readings are clear. Examples: "I doubt that the worker is patient when someone needs time." and "I am still unsure whether the worker is patient when someone needs time."
- Özlem says when to call back: "Call after Monday 2 November, 11:18".

**Unclear**
- The weighing paragraph made me reread on both devices. Quoted: "She weighs each account by how strongly it spoke and by how much she trusted the speaker when she heard it, for one quality of one person at a time. One account is evidence, not proof."
- The half-circle (◐) and the empty circle (◌) beside names on the phone bench. I guessed they show waiting time. Nothing on screen told me what they mean.
- "Call in to the office / The journey takes time." I understood it only after reading Özlem's and Gülce's own lines.

I did not reach the agreement, Families, Placed or Negotiate screens in these sessions, so I did not count them.

## 6. Device use

**Phone (360x690, touch)**
- I tapped named controls. Four screenshots were taken: a-001 (title), a-002 (the placed office at 09:45), and a-003, which I looked at on the Feedback panel. I viewed all three; the third automatic shot is a-003.
- On a-003 the Feedback buttons are large, readable and easy to hit with a finger.
- Typing the name "I'm confused" failed in the shell, so I tapped by coordinates (271, 184) from the screenshot. That is my quoting problem, not a game fault.

**Desktop (1280x800, keyboard and mouse)**
- Two shots viewed: b-001 (title: centred column, large type, red "Open the office" button) and b-002 (Feedback panel).
- On b-002 the panel opens over the office picture, and the picture is cut off behind it. The panel is readable. I tapped "I'm stopping" by coordinates (505, 207).
- I saw no trouble with layout at either size beyond the cut-off picture behind the panel. I did not inspect other layouts on the phone beyond those three shots.

## 7. Stop and return

**Phone stop:** "I stop here for tonight on the phone. The long weighing paragraph made me reread twice. The story is still clear and I would come back." Reason: CONFUSED. Before it, I opened Game menu → Feedback, chose "I'm confused", pressed Back and sent nothing.

**Desktop stop:** "I stop on the desktop after about 85 seconds. The long weighing paragraph again tires me and I have seen the same loop as on the phone. I did not reach a placement here." Reason: CONFUSED. Before it, I opened Game menu → Feedback, chose "I'm stopping", pressed Back and sent nothing. Both labels were present on both devices.

**Would you return voluntarily?** Yes, another evening, on the big screen. I would come back to see whether Özlem or the Kaya family gets a good match. My condition is that the paragraph about weighing answers is cut to one short sentence. If it stays as long as it is now, I would still come back, but I would stop reading each card after the first line, and I might not get far. I would not leave it running; I would close it, because the game needs my choices and I read slowly.

## 8. Changes I would want

1. Shorten the weighing paragraph under each quality to one short sentence, or hide it behind a "Why?" button.
2. Collapse the six "You have not asked yet." lines into one line on a new applicant card.
3. After a placement, say plainly what to do while waiting, and say what the ◐ and ◌ marks mean on the bench.

## 9. Compared with my last visit

**First Good Hands report**
- **Ask 1, make "Nermin's reading" say why:** Met. Every reading now gives a short reason, such as "I doubt that the worker is patient when someone needs time". The paragraph under it is too long, but the reason is there.
- **Ask 2, rewrite the fee and deposit paragraph with real numbers:** Not seen. I never reached Negotiate in either session.
- **Ask 3, one short line instead of the same sentence in five boxes; fix the cut-off Next line and the large picture on the phone:** Partly met. The Next line on the phone read in full at 68 s. The "You have not asked yet." sentence still repeats six times on a new card. I did not re-check the size of the picture beyond a-002.

**Retest1 report**
- **Ask 1, tell me what to do while I wait after a placement:** Partly met. On the phone, the Next line said I can check in by phone on a placed worker at Tuesday 18:20, which is a hint. After that I read "nothing else to do" and skipped. I did not see a fuller instruction.
- **Ask 2, say each wage and fee once on the agreement screen:** Not seen. I did not reach the agreement.
- **Ask 3, keep "I'm stopping" and "I'm confused" in Feedback on both devices:** Met. Both buttons were there on both devices.

**New this time**
- The long weighing paragraph is new on the card and made me reread on both devices. It is the one thing that would stop me returning if it stays as it is.

## 2026-10-31 hearsay-retest2 — free stop

Source: [readable report view](../reviews/light-2026-10-30/hearsay-retest2/rosa/report.md), [exact producer original](../reviews/light-2026-10-30/hearsay-retest2/rosa/report.original.md.txt), original SHA256 `b02bd0040ae814211bd7d7b99b68fcb7d373ecc6ab02de41c9a269e5db9e5a51`. Method qualification: [rosa](../reviews/light-2026-10-30/hearsay-retest2/rosa/director-method-qualification.json). Codex producer reports a full history read; complete command stdout coverage is verified, but delivery to the model and comprehension are not verified. Image viewing is self-reported and unverified.

**Readable view of the producer report follows. The linked original preserves the exact bytes.**

I would come back another evening on the big screen if the game told me why someone cannot talk and when they may be free; I would not leave it running, and I would not return on the phone just to wait.

**Ranked asks**

1. Say why someone at the garden cannot talk and, if known, when they will be free.
2. Say when people return after the garden empties.
3. Shorten the repeated Pace explanation.

## 1. Session

I am Rosa Alvarez, 62, a retired school administrator from Puebla and a busy second-language English reader. This was a returning-player recovery in **light-1031-hearsay-retest2**, free-stop arm, floor 0. The two visits were fresh, independent games at `https://hearsay.adamwhite.work/?labRunSource=agent`.

- **Phone A:** 360×690 touch, light mode. Stop logged at **43.250 seconds**.
- **Desktop B:** 1280×800, keyboard and mouse, light mode. Stop logged at **30.128 seconds**. I used mouse actions, not keyboard keys.

I read all 2,311 supplied history lines. The last line was: **“End of supplied own history. This reading boundary is director metadata, not an event you experienced.”** Exact unread range: **none**.

The history is a selection, not my complete archive. Older records and independent continued alternatives were not supplied. The excluded Warren alternative body and older Well raw diary were not supplied. Earlier method limits remain: my Hearsay retest1 history read stopped at 826 of 1,419 lines; my Good Hands retest1 read stopped at 468 of 1,682; my Pass retest2 read stopped at 399 of 1,583. My earlier Hearsay desktop TOOL stop did not establish a broken game selector.

The supplied current-build phone attempt was **cut by the coordinator at 60.268 seconds**, not voluntarily stopped. Its desktop context was never opened. This recovery is in the same second retest, after a backend change and repeated exposure; the supplied note says no game fix occurred between attempts. I do not assign a stop reason or outcome to the cut attempt.

Recording remained on. I changed no consent, typed no feedback note and pressed no Submit. Neither session reached the first-minute or pulse checkpoint.

## 2. First impressions

On the phone, the familiar title and Day 13 goal were clear. At 7.736 seconds, Rıza’s blue doorway reminded me of his photographs. At 11.222 seconds, the sketches and daughter confirmed the familiar story for me. On desktop, the title was familiar again; the folded conversation heading opened, and both lists accepted my choices.

## 3. Verbatim diary

These are the reaction strings I actually sent, in order, with driver elapsed seconds. Actions without a reaction are omitted.

**Phone**

- **6.205:** “I know this title and the Day 13 lunch from before. I begin again.”
- **7.736:** “Riza asks about the blue doorway. I remember his photographs, so I invite him.”
- **11.222:** “The doorway sketches and his daughter are the same kind story I remember. I am confident I recognise it. I help privately.”
- **12.736:** “Osman helps with bread. I want to speak to him.”
- **17.472:** “He kept the tenant money. I want him to put it right, quietly.”
- **20.534:** “Derya and her training letter remind me of her earlier story. I ask her.”
- **24.838:** “I remember the reported error and lost training. I am confident this is Derya from before. I help her quietly.”
- **31.818:** “Nermin runs an agency, like the woman I remember from Good Hands. I think it may be her, but the name and agency alone do not prove it.”
- **33.597, want_quit, CONFUSED, Day 1 07:10:** “Nermin cannot talk and I do not know when she will be free. I understand the words but still do not know why. I have had three quiet talks and would stop here.”
- **43.250, stop, CONFUSED, Day 1 07:14:** “I liked helping three people quietly. Nermin cannot talk and I do not know why or when she will be free. I stop on the phone.”

**Desktop**

- **5.246:** “Same familiar title on the big screen. I begin a fresh garden.”
- **7.241:** “I remember this folded heading and want to ask rather than accuse.”
- **15.344:** “The lists accepted Osman and honesty. I ask Riza what he has heard.”
- **16.874:** “He has not heard enough and asks to talk about something else. That is clear; I listen.”
- **20.628:** “The same doorway photographs and daughter again, as on the phone and before. I keep his confidence.”
- **22.243, want_quit, BORED, Day 1 06:29:** “I have heard the same gentle story again and the lists work. I would stop for tonight rather than wait for Day 4.”
- **30.128, stop, BORED, Day 1 06:32:** “The lists worked and Riza answered clearly. I liked his story again, but I have seen it tonight and would stop rather than wait for Day 4.”

## 4. Moments

The quiet stories held me. I could help Rıza plan a public walking route, help Derya word a request, and see Osman write a proposed repayment letter. The game was careful to say that the visit, reply and repayment had not happened yet.

Nermin’s refusal brought back my earlier question. The words “You do not know when Nermin will be free” were clear, but I did not know why she could not talk while shown at the garden. My phone quit wish did not pass. On desktop, I liked Rıza’s plain answer, but after hearing his familiar story again I wanted to stop for the evening. That quit wish did not pass either.

There were no pulses: both sessions stopped before 180 seconds. The first-minute checkpoint was also untested on both.

## 5. Confusion and clarity

I did not need to reread a sentence in either session. The two-reread rule did not trigger. The Day 4 notice clearly said when the supply offer would arrive and that nothing could be ordered before then. Rıza’s answer that he had not heard enough to judge Osman was clear.

“Condition” remained unclear to me. Nermin’s availability was also unclear in substance, even though the refusal itself was plain. I did not reach an empty garden, Day 4, or the lunch.

## 7. Stop and return

The phone stop was **CONFUSED at 43.250 seconds**: “I liked helping three people quietly. Nermin cannot talk and I do not know why or when she will be free. I stop on the phone.”

The desktop stop was **BORED at 30.128 seconds**: “The lists worked and Riza answered clearly. I liked his story again, but I have seen it tonight and would stop rather than wait for Day 4.”

I would return voluntarily another evening on the big screen if the game told me why someone cannot talk and when they may be free. I still want to see the bread offer and what happens to these people. I would not leave it running: time moves and I would not know what I missed. I would not return on the phone merely to wait.
